\displaystyle C\left|\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x}\frac{\Delta\theta}{\Delta x}\right|(\Delta x)^{2}G_{0}(\Delta x,2\sigma)^{-1}((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left|\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x}\frac{\Delta\theta}{\Delta x}\right|\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-1}.

In the same manner, we have

(3.37)

|e_{g}|\leq C\left|\frac{\Delta g}{\Delta x}\frac{\Delta\theta}{\Delta x}\right|\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-1}.

In particular, it follows

|e_{f}|+|e_{g}|\leq Cw_{0}^{2}(\Delta x)^{-2}.

Recall that
\partial_{x_{1}}P_{12}=K_{12}+J_{12}=\tilde{K}_{12}+\tilde{J}_{12}
, so

\displaystyle\left|K_{12}-\tilde{K}_{12}\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{J}_{12}-J_{12}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2(\Delta g+2\sigma+\theta(x))(\partial_{x}g(x_{1})\Delta x-\Delta g)}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|+\left|\frac{2(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))(\partial_{x}g(x_{1})\Delta x-\Delta f)}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cw_{0}(\Delta x)^{-2},

and in the same way we have
\left|K_{21}-\tilde{K}_{21}\right|\leq Cw_{0}(\Delta x)^{-2}.

∎

Proof of Proposition
3.6
.

With the help of (
3.25
), (
3.27
) and (
3.28
), (
3.19
)(
3.20
) yield that

\Re\left(\frac{1}{2}K_{g}+D_{11}-\mu_{2}^{2}K_{f}-\mu_{1}^{2}K_{g}\right)\geq\left(\frac{7}{16}-Cw_{0}\right)(\Delta x)^{-2},

\Re\left(\frac{1}{2}K_{f}+D_{11}-\mu_{2}^{2}K_{f}-\mu_{1}^{2}K_{g}\right)\geq\left(\frac{7}{16}-Cw_{0}\right)(\Delta x)^{-2}.

Then from (
3.23
)(
3.24
)(
3.26
), it follows from (
3.18
) that

(3.38)

\displaystyle\Re D_{11}\Re D_{22}-(\Re D_{12})^{2}\geq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}-Cw_{0}\right)(\Re K_{f}+\Re K_{g})\cdot\left(\frac{7}{8}-Cw_{0}\right)(\Delta x)^{-2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{7}{16}-Cw_{0}\right)\Re D_{22}\Re D_{11}.

Finally, by (
3.26
)(
3.35
)(
3.38
) we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Re D_{11}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Re D_{12}\Re\left(\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\Re D_{22}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Re D_{11}\left|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\frac{\Re D_{12}}{\Re D_{11}}\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\right|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{2}\left(\Re D_{22}-\frac{(\Re D_{12})^{2}}{\Re D_{11}}\right)|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Re D_{11}\left(\left|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\frac{\Re D_{12}}{\Re D_{11}}\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\right|^{2}+\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\Re D_{11}\Re D_{22}-(\Re D_{12})^{2}}{2(\Re D_{11})^{2}}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{4}\left(\Re D_{22}-\frac{(\Re D_{12})^{2}}{\Re D_{11}}\right)|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Re D_{11}\left(1+\frac{2(\Re D_{12})^{2}}{\Re D_{11}\Re D_{22}-(\Re D_{12})^{2}}\right)^{-1}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{4}\left(\Re D_{22}-\frac{(\Re D_{12})^{2}}{\Re D_{11}}\right)|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{18}{7}+Cw_{0}\right)^{-1}\Re D_{11}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}+\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\frac{7}{64}-Cw_{0}\right)\Re D_{22}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{7}{50}-Cw_{0}\right)D_{11}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}+\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\frac{7}{64}-Cw_{0}\right)D_{22}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
3.2
.

To obtain the control of the integral of quadratic form (
3.15
), it remains to bound the terms involving
e_{f}
and
e_{g}
on the right-hand side of (
3.16
), for which we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Re e_{f}(x,x_{1})\cdot\Re\left(\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}\right)dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x}\frac{\Delta\theta}{\Delta x}\right|\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-1}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

where we used
\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{-1}d\alpha=C\sigma^{-1}
and

|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|\leq|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|+|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})|
,
|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|\leq|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|+|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x_{1})|
.
As analogues to the above inequality, we also have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Re e_{g}(x,x_{1})\Re\left(\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}\right)dxdx_{1}\right|\lesssim\frac{\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}}{\sigma}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Re\left(e_{f}+e_{g}\right)(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right|\lesssim\frac{\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}}{\sigma}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

Collecting the above bounds and using Proposition
3.6
with a small enough
w_{0}
yields that

(3.39)

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left\{\Re D_{11}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{2}\Re\left(2K_{11}-K_{12}-K_{21}+2K_{22}-\tilde{K}_{12}-\tilde{K}_{21}\right)|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Re\left(\mu_{2}\left(2K_{11}-K_{12}-K_{21}\right)-\mu_{1}\left(2K_{22}-\tilde{K}_{12}-\tilde{K}_{21}\right)\right)\Re\left(\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}\right)\right\}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle C\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{11}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}dxdx_{1}+C\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle-C\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle-C\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

∎

3.3.
Lower order terms

In this part, we give the control over
T_{low}
, i.e. the terms involving
J_{ij}
and
\tilde{J}_{ij}
,
(i,j)\in\left\{1,2\right\}^{2}
in (
3.4
)(
3.6
) and (
3.9
-
3.12
). These terms are considered lower order for that
J_{ij}
and
\tilde{J}_{ij}
have
\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x
or
\Delta g-\partial_{x}g(x_{1})\Delta x
as a factor, which contributes
(\Delta x)^{2}
when
\Delta x
is close to
0
:

\left|f(x)-f(x_{1})-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x\right|=\left|\int_{x_{1}}^{x}\partial_{x}^{2}f(y)(x-y)dy\right|\leq(\Delta x)^{2}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1}).

Lemma 3.8
.

Suppose that condition (
3.17
) holds. Then

\displaystyle|T_{low}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{11}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}dxdx_{1}+\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right).

In the remainder of this section, we assume that the solution satisfies (
3.17
) so that

|J_{11}|\leq 2\left|\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x}\right|\left|\frac{\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\right|\left|\frac{(\Delta x)^{3}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f)^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\Delta x|}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})M[\partial_{x}f](x_{1}),

|J_{22}|\leq 2\left|\frac{\Delta g}{\Delta x}\right|\left|\frac{\Delta g-\partial_{x}g(x_{1})\Delta x}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\right|\left|\frac{(\Delta x)^{3}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta g)^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\Delta x|}M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x_{1})M[\partial_{x}g](x_{1}).

Here we denote
M[v]
the Hardy-Littlewood maximal function of
v
for an arbitrary function
v
. Then using Hölder’s inequality we obtain

(3.40)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}J_{11}(x,x_{1})\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}M[\partial_{x}f](x_{1})M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\Delta x}\right|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\Delta x}\right|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}M[\partial_{x}f](x_{1})^{2}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})^{2}dx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}},

(3.41)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}J_{22}(x,x_{1})\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

To control
J_{12}+J_{21}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{2(\Delta f-2\sigma-\theta(x))}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\Delta f+\Delta g|\left|\frac{1}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle+|4\sigma+\theta(x_{1})+\theta(x)|\left|\frac{1}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{1}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\Delta f+\Delta g|}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2\Delta x\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}.

Hence

|J_{12}+J_{21}|\lesssim\frac{M[\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g](x_{1})M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})}{|\Delta x|}+\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}},

and as an analogue,

|\tilde{J}_{12}+\tilde{J}_{21}|\lesssim\frac{M[\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g](x_{1})M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x_{1})}{|\Delta x|}+\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}.

Therefore, we get

(3.42)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(J_{12}+J_{21}+\tilde{J}_{12}+\tilde{J}_{21})(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}M[\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g](x_{1})\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})+M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x_{1})\right)\frac{|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|}{|\Delta x|}|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h||\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{2}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Since we do not have enough dissipation in
\theta
, it is necessary to carefully make use of the cancellation within
J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}
and
J_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{21}
to control the terms involving
J_{ij}
and
\tilde{J}_{ij}
in (
3.6
) and (
3.9
-
3.12
).

Lemma 3.9
.

Suppose that (
3.17
) holds, then for
(x,x_{1})\in\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{2}

\displaystyle\left|J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\Delta f||\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\cdot\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}+\frac{|\Delta\theta||\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|}{(\Delta x)^{2}((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})}+\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})||\Delta x|(|\Delta f|+\sigma)}{((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}},

\displaystyle\left|J_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{21}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\Delta g||\Delta g-\partial_{x}g(x_{1})\Delta x|}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\cdot\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}+\frac{|\Delta\theta||\Delta g-\partial_{x}g(x_{1})\Delta x|}{(\Delta x)^{2}((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})}+\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x)||\Delta x|(|\Delta g|+\sigma)}{((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}.

Proof.

Denote

\displaystyle\tilde{G}\left(\Delta x,2\sigma;\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f)^{2}\right)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(-\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)^{2}

We compute by brute force:

\displaystyle\frac{2\Delta f}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f)^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1})}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{\Delta f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(-\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{G}\left(\Delta x,2\sigma;\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)^{-1}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{8}\tilde{c}_{j}\left(\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)(\Delta x)^{j}(2\sigma)^{9-j},

where the coefficients
c_{j}
are given by

\tilde{c}_{1}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=2w_{1}(1+w_{2})^{2}(1+w_{3})^{2},

\tilde{c}_{2}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=8w_{1}^{2}(1+w_{2})^{3}(1+w_{3})^{3}(w_{2}-w_{3}),

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{3}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=

\displaystyle 4w_{1}(1+w_{2})^{2}(1+w_{3})^{2}(1+3w_{1}^{2})\left[(1+w_{2})^{2}+(1+w_{3})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-32w_{1}^{3}(1+w_{2})^{3}(1+w_{3})^{3},

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{4}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=

\displaystyle-(1+w_{1}^{2})(1-7w_{1}^{2})(1+w_{2})(1+w_{3})\left[(1+w_{2})^{3}-(1+w_{3})^{3}\right]

\displaystyle+6w_{1}^{2}(1+3w_{1}^{2})(1+w_{2})^{2}(1+w_{3})^{2}(w_{2}-w_{3}),

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{5}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=

\displaystyle w_{1}(1+w_{1}^{2})^{2}\left[(1+w_{2})^{4}+(1+w_{3})^{4}\right]+8w_{1}(1+3w_{1}^{2})(1+w_{2})^{2}(1+w_{3})^{2}

\displaystyle+4w_{1}(1+w_{1}^{2})(1-7w_{1}^{2})(1+w_{2})(1+w_{3})\left[(1+w_{2})^{2}+(1+w_{3})^{2}\right],

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{6}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=

\displaystyle 4w_{1}^{2}(1+w_{1}^{2})^{2}\left[(1+w_{3})^{3}-(1+w_{2})^{3}\right]

\displaystyle-2(1+w_{1}^{2})(1+3w_{1}^{2})(1-7w_{1}^{2})(1+w_{2})(1+w_{3})(w_{2}-w_{3}),

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{7}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=

\displaystyle 2w_{1}(1+3w_{1}^{2})(1+w_{1}^{2})^{2}\left[(1+w_{2})^{2}+(1+w_{3})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4w_{1}(1+w_{1}^{2})^{2}(1-7w_{1}^{2})(1+w_{2})(1+w_{3}),

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{8}(w_{1},w_{2},w_{3}):=(w_{2}-w_{3})(1+w_{1}^{2})^{3}(1-3w_{1}^{2}).

By virtue of (
3.17
), the following controls hold:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{4}\left|\tilde{c}_{2j-1}\left(\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)(\Delta x)^{2j-1}(2\sigma)^{10-2j}\right|\lesssim|\Delta f|(2\sigma)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{3},

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{4}\left|\tilde{c}_{2j}\left(\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)(\Delta x)^{2j}(2\sigma)^{9-2j}\right|\lesssim|\Delta\theta|(\Delta x)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{3},

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{G}\left(\Delta x,2\sigma;\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)\right|\simeq(\Delta x)^{4}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{4}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x\right|\left|\tilde{G}\left(\Delta x,2\sigma;\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)\right|^{-1}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{8}\tilde{c}_{j}\left(\frac{\Delta f}{\Delta x},\frac{\theta(x)}{2\sigma},\frac{\theta(x_{1})}{2\sigma}\right)(\Delta x)^{j}(2\sigma)^{9-j}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{2\Delta x\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\Delta f||\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\cdot\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}+\frac{|\Delta\theta||\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|}{(\Delta x)^{2}((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})}+\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})||\Delta x|(|\Delta f|+\sigma)}{((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}.

Replacing
f
by
g
and swapping
\theta(x)
with
\theta(x_{1})
yields the desired control of
\left|J_{22}-\tilde{J}_{12}-\tilde{J}_{21}\right|
.
∎

Using Lemma
3.9
and Hölder inequality, we estimate the lower order terms in (
3.6
):

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x\right|\frac{|\Delta f|}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta||\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x\right|\frac{|\Delta\theta|}{(\Delta x)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta||\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})||\Delta x|(|\Delta f|+\sigma)}{((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta||\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}{\Delta x}\right|^{2}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta f|^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta\theta|^{2}}{(2\sigma)^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)}|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|^{2}|\Delta x|^{4}(|\Delta f|+\sigma)^{2}}{\sigma^{2}((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right].

For each
x\in\Gamma_{\pm}(t)
,

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta f|^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}dx_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})^{2}M[\partial_{x}f](x_{1})^{2}dx_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta\theta|^{2}}{(2\sigma)^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)}dx_{1}\leq\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})^{2}\left|\frac{\Delta\theta}{2\sigma}\right|^{2}dx_{1}\lesssim\sigma^{-2}\left\|\theta\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|^{2}|\Delta x|^{4}(|\Delta f|+\sigma)^{2}}{\sigma^{2}((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}dx_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\sigma^{-2}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(M[\partial_{x}f](x_{1})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\right)dx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-2}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\sigma\right).

Therefore,

(3.43)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\left\|\theta\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

By replacing
J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}
by
J_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{21}
or replacing
\partial_{x}^{k}\theta
by
\partial_{x}^{k}h
, we can also obtain

(3.44)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\left\|\theta\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(3.45)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\left\|\theta\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(3.46)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\left\|\theta\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For the lower order terms in (
3.11
)(
3.12
), we write

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x\right|\frac{|\Delta f|}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h||\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x\right|\frac{|\Delta\theta|}{(\Delta x)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h||\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})||\Delta x|(|\Delta f|+\sigma)}{((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h||\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\Delta x}\right|dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta f|^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{4}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta\theta|^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|^{2}|\Delta x|^{4}(|\Delta f|+\sigma)^{2}}{((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{4}}|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right].

For any
x\in\Gamma_{\pm}(t)
we have the following controls:

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta f|^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{4}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}dx_{1}\leq\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\Delta f-\partial_{x}f(x_{1})\Delta x|^{2}|\Delta\theta|^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}dx_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x_{1})^{2}M[\partial_{x}\theta](x_{1})^{2}dx_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|^{2}|\Delta x|^{4}(|\Delta f|+\sigma)^{2}}{((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{4}}dx_{1}\lesssim\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{|\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})|^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}dx_{1}\lesssim\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

Therefore,

(3.47)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{11}-\frac{1}{2}J_{12}-\frac{1}{2}J_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right]\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}},

and likewise

(3.48)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{J}_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right]\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Summing up (
3.40
-
3.48
), we arrive at Lemma
3.8
.

3.4.
Anti-symmetric part

In this part, we give the control of the last terms on the right-hand side of (
3.9
-
3.12
) involving
P_{12}-P_{21}
. The main result of this section is the following.

Lemma 3.10
.

Suppose that condition (
3.17
) holds. The anti-symmetric part
T_{asym}
satisfies

\displaystyle|T_{asym}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\frac{\partial_{x}\theta}{2\sigma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Proof.

To begin with, we recall the definition of
P_{12}
and
P_{21}
to get

P_{12}(x,x_{1})-P_{21}(x,x_{1})=-\frac{\Delta x(4\sigma+\theta(x)+\theta(x_{1}))(\Delta f+\Delta g)}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(-\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)},

and under the condition (
3.17
)

\displaystyle\partial_{x_{1}}\left(P_{12}(x,x_{1})-P_{21}(x,x_{1})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta x(\Delta f+\Delta g)\partial_{x}\theta(x_{1})-(4\sigma+\theta(x)+\theta(x_{1}))\left(\Delta x(\partial_{x}f(x_{1})+\partial_{x}g(x_{1}))+\Delta f+\Delta g\right)}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(-\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\Delta x(4\sigma+\theta(x)+\theta(x_{1}))(\Delta f+\Delta g)}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(-\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\frac{2\Delta x+2\partial_{x}g(x_{1})(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}))^{2}\right)}+\frac{2\Delta x+2\partial_{x}f(x_{1})(\Delta f-2\sigma-\theta(x))}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(-\Delta f+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left[\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\frac{(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma|\Delta x|}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma(\Delta x)^{2}(|\Delta x|+|2\sigma|)}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{3}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left[\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\frac{(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma|\Delta x|}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}\right].

Note that
\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq w_{0}
. By Young’s inequality, it follows that

(3.49)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\partial_{x_{1}}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left[\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(\frac{2\sigma|\Delta x|+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|\Delta x|^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}\right)|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})|dx_{1}\right]|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}(2\sigma)^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

where we used that
\left\|\frac{\alpha^{2}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}\right\|_{L^{1}}\simeq\left\|\frac{2\sigma\alpha}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}\right\|_{L^{1}}\simeq(2\sigma)^{-1}.

Likewise, for the terms involving
P_{12}-P_{21}
in (
3.11
)(
3.12
) we have

(3.50)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k+1}h(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}(2\sigma)^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

However, the bounds (
3.49
)(
3.50
) are not uniform in
\sigma
for
\sigma
close to
0
. In order to obtain a uniform estimate, we write
\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta=-\Lambda H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta
, where
H
is the Hilbert transform, and
\Lambda:=H\partial_{x}.
Note that

\Lambda v(x)=\frac{1}{\pi}P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{v(x)-v(x-\alpha)}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha
for any Schwartz class function
v
.
Using this identity, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\Lambda H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}P.V.\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})}{\alpha^{2}}\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}d\alpha dxdx_{1}.

Then apply a change of variables by setting
\alpha=-\tilde{\alpha}
,
x_{1}=\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{\alpha}
,
x=\tilde{x}-\tilde{\alpha}
:

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})}{\alpha^{2}}\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}d\alpha dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{12}-P_{21})(\tilde{x}-\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{\alpha})\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(\tilde{x}_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{\alpha})}{\tilde{\alpha}^{2}}\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(\tilde{x}-\tilde{\alpha})}d\tilde{\alpha}d\tilde{x}d\tilde{x_{1}}.

Hence

(3.51)

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi}P.V.\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{(H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha))\overline{(\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha))}}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi}P.V.\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})-(P_{12}-P_{21})(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}d\alpha dxdx_{1}.

For the first term on the right-hand side, we see from (
3.17
) that

|P_{12}(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)-P_{21}(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\frac{2\sigma(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}.

Hence by Young’s inequality and the
L^{2}
boundedness of the Hilbert transform, it follows

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}dx_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\pm}(t)\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\sigma(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}\left|\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|dx_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\pm}(t)\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\pm}(t)\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Then by Hölder’s inequality we obtain

(3.52)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)\right.

\displaystyle\cdot\left.\frac{(H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha))\overline{(\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha))}}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}}.

To control
(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})-(P_{12}-P_{21})(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)
, we have the following estimates:

|(4\sigma+\theta(x)+\theta(x_{1}))-(4\sigma+\theta(x-\alpha)+\theta(x_{1}-\alpha))|\leq 2\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|\alpha|,

\displaystyle\left|\left(f(x)-f(x_{1})+g(x)-g(x_{1})\right)-\left(f(x-\alpha)-f(x_{1}-\alpha)+g(x-\alpha)-g(x_{1}-\alpha\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{x_{1}}^{x}\int_{0}^{\alpha}\left(\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\right)(y-\beta)d\beta dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|x-x_{1}||\alpha|,

\displaystyle\left|\alpha^{2}+\left(f(x)-f(x_{1})+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1})\right)^{2}-\alpha^{2}-\left(f(x-\alpha)-f(x_{1}-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|f(x)+f(x-\alpha)-f(x_{1})-f(x_{1}-\alpha)+4\sigma+\theta(x_{1})+\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\cdot\left|f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-f(x_{1})+f(x_{1}-\alpha)+\theta(x_{1})-\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left|f(x)+f(x-\alpha)-f(x_{1})-f(x_{1}-\alpha)+4\sigma+\theta(x_{1})+\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\cdot\left|\int_{x_{1}}^{x}\int_{0}^{\alpha}\left(\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\right)(y-\beta)d\beta dy+\theta(x)+\theta(x_{1})-\theta(x-\alpha)-\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|x-x_{1}|+4\sigma+2\|\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|x-x_{1}|+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)|\alpha|,

and likewise

\displaystyle\left|\alpha^{2}+\left(f(x_{1})-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)\right)^{2}-\alpha^{2}-\left(f(x_{1}-\alpha)-f(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x-\alpha)\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|x-x_{1}|+4\sigma+2\|\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|x-x_{1}|+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)|\alpha|.

Using Lemma
3.5
and (
3.17
), we obtain

(3.53)

\displaystyle\left|(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})-(P_{12}-P_{21})(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\frac{\partial_{x}\theta}{2\sigma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}|\alpha|}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}.

To control the second term on the right-hand side of (
3.51
) we decompose the integral into the region
|\alpha|\leq 1
and
|\alpha|>1
. In the region
|\alpha|\leq 1
, we use

(3.54)

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}\left|\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right||\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dxdx_{1}d\alpha

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\left|\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|^{2}dx_{1}d\alpha\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dx\right)^{2}dx_{1}d\alpha\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

In the region
|\alpha|>1
, we use

(3.55)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})-(P_{12}-P_{21})(x-\alpha,x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}\frac{H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})-H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}\left(|H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})|+|H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)|\right).

Then by Hölder and Young’s inequality

(3.56)

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}|H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})||\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dxdx_{1}d\alpha

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dx\right)|H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1})|dx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

(3.57)

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}|H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)||\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dxdx_{1}d\alpha

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\frac{(2\sigma)(\Delta x)^{2}}{\left((\Delta x)^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|dx\right)\left(\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}|H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x_{1}-\alpha)|d\alpha\right)dx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Collecting the controls in (
3.52
-
3.57
), we conclude that

(3.58)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\frac{\partial_{x}\theta}{2\sigma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

As an analogue, the terms involving
P_{12}-P_{21}
in (
3.11
)(
3.12
) satisfy the control

(3.59)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k+1}h(x_{1})\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}dxdx_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f+\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\frac{\partial_{x}\theta}{2\sigma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

∎

3.5.
Transport terms

The contribution of the transport terms in (
3.1
)(
3.2
) to the energy estimate is as follows:

\displaystyle-\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(\mu_{2}u_{+}+\mu_{1}u_{-})\partial_{x}^{k+1}h\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(\mu_{1}u_{+}+\mu_{2}u_{-})\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}\,dx

\displaystyle-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(u_{+}-u_{-})(\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}+\partial_{x}^{k+1}h\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta})dx.

We remark that
u_{+}
and
u_{-}
are not necessarily real-valued, so integration by parts is not applicable. The aim of this section is to bound the transport terms by losing at most half a derivative in
h
and
\theta
, see Lemma
3.12
.
To this end, for
u=u_{+}
or
u=u_{-}
we write

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}u\partial_{x}^{k+1}h\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx\right|=\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\ u\Lambda H\partial_{x}^{k}h\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx\right|=\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h})\cdot\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}H\partial_{x}^{k}h\,dx\right|,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}u\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx\right|=\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\ u\Lambda H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx\right|=\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h})\cdot\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}H\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\,dx\right|,

and subsequently

\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}u\partial_{x}^{k+1}h\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx\right|\leq\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}_{\gamma(t)}^{\frac{1}{2}}},

\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}u\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx\right|\leq\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}_{\gamma(t)}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

The main issue is to find a control for
\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}
. To start with, we have

\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}u\|_{L^{6}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{3}_{\gamma(t)}}.

To bound
\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}u\|_{L^{6}_{\gamma(t))}}
, we use the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality

\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}u\|_{L^{6}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\|u\|_{L^{12}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Lambda u\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and the following Lemma.

Lemma 3.11
.

Suppose the condition (
3.17
) holds, then for any
p>1

\|u_{+}\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|u_{-}\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

and for
p>2

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{+}\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}u_{-}\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Proof.

Recall the expression of
u_{+}
and
u_{-}
(
2.9
)(
2.10
). It suffices to consider only
u_{+}
since the proof of the bound for
u_{-}
is almost a repetition. We begin with the first term of (
2.9
). Using the cancellation between the integral over the region
\alpha<0
and the region
\alpha>0
, we write

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha(f(x+\alpha)-f(x-\alpha))(f(x-\alpha)+f(x+\alpha)-2f(x))}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2}\right)\left(\alpha^{2}+(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))^{2}\right)}d\alpha

For the part
\alpha>1
, it holds

(3.60)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\alpha>1}\frac{\alpha(f(x+\alpha)-f(x-\alpha))(f(x-\alpha)+f(x+\alpha)-2f(x))}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2}\right)\left(\alpha^{2}+(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))^{2}\right)}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\alpha>1}\frac{1}{\alpha}\left|\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|\left|\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)}{\alpha}-\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}f](x)\int_{\alpha>1}\frac{M[\partial_{x}f](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}f](x-\alpha)}{\alpha}d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}f](x)\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

where we used

|f(x+\alpha)-f(x-\alpha)|\leq 2\alpha M[\partial_{x}f](x),

|f(x+\alpha)-f(x)|\leq\alpha M[\partial_{x}f](x+a),\quad|f(x-\alpha)-f(x)|\leq\alpha M[\partial_{x}f](x-\alpha).

For the part
0<\alpha<1
, to cancel the singularity
\alpha^{-1}
in the integrand, we use the inequality

\displaystyle\left|f(x-\alpha)+f(x+\alpha)-2f(x)\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-\alpha}^{\alpha}(\alpha-|\beta|)\partial_{x}^{2}f(x+\beta)d\beta\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{2}(M[\partial^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)),

and thus

(3.61)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0<\alpha<1}\frac{\alpha(f(x+\alpha)-f(x-\alpha))(f(x-\alpha)+f(x+\alpha)-2f(x))}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2}\right)\left(\alpha^{2}+(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))^{2}\right)}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0<\alpha<1}M[\partial_{x}f](x)(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha))d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}f](x)\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Hence
\left|P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2}}d\alpha\right|\leq C\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}M[\partial_{x}f](x)
.
For the second term of (
2.9
),

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma)^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha\left(\left(g(x)-g(x+\alpha)^{2}+2\sigma+\theta(x)\right)^{2}-\left(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x)\right)^{2}\right)}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x+\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha(g(x-\alpha)-g(x+\alpha))(2g(x)-g(x-\alpha)-g(x+\alpha))}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x+\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)}d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha(4\sigma+2\theta(x))(g(x-\alpha)-g(x+\alpha))}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x+\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)}d\alpha.

Since
|\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x\pm\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}|^{-1}\leq C(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{-1}\leq C\alpha^{-2}
, the first term on the right-hand side of the above identity can be bounded in absolute value by
CM[\partial_{x}g](x)\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}
in the same way as in (
3.60
)(
3.61
). For the second term, in view of the condition (
3.17
), it holds

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha(4\sigma+2\theta(x))(g(x-\alpha)-g(x+\alpha))}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)\left(\alpha^{2}+(g(x)-g(x+\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right)}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}g](x)\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha^{2}(2\sigma)}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}g](x).

Taking the sum yields

\left|P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma)^{2}}d\alpha\right|\lesssim(1+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}})M[\partial_{x}g](x),
and consequently

|u_{+}(x)|\lesssim(1+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}})(M[\partial_{x}f](x)+M[\partial_{x}g](x)).

As an analogue,
u_{-}
satisfies the bound

|u_{-}(x)|\lesssim(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}})(M[\partial_{x}f](x)+M[\partial_{x}g](x)).

By the
L^{p}
boundedness of the maximal function for
p>1
we obtain the desired bound for
\|u_{+}\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|u_{-}\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}
.
To obtain the estimate of
\partial_{x}u_{\pm}
, we take derivative in (
2.9
) to get

(3.62)

\displaystyle\partial_{x}u_{+}=

\displaystyle\mu_{2}\Delta\rho\,P.V\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha\Delta f(x,x-\alpha)\Delta\partial_{x}f(x,x-\alpha)}{(\alpha^{2}+(\Delta f(x,x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle+\mu_{1}\Delta\rho\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(\Delta g(x,x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))(\Delta\partial_{x}g(x,x-\alpha)+\partial_{x}\theta(x))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha.

Again, we divide the integrals into the region
|\alpha|>1
and the region
|\alpha|<1
. When
|\alpha|>1
, it holds

(3.63)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{2\alpha(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))(\partial_{x}f(x)-\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(\Delta f(x,x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)}{|\alpha|}d\alpha.

Since
p>2
, Young’s inequality gives

\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{1}{\alpha}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\||\alpha|^{-1}\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|>1}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{p+2}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

When
|\alpha|<1
, we write

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|\alpha|<1}\frac{2\alpha(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))(\partial_{x}f(x)-\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(\Delta f(x,x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0<\alpha<1}\left|\frac{2\alpha(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))(\partial_{x}f(x)-\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{2\alpha(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))(\partial_{x}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}f(x))}{(\alpha^{2}+(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))^{2})^{2}}\right|d\alpha.

Using Lemma (
3.5
) with the inequalities

|(\partial_{x}f(x)-\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha))-(\partial_{x}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}f(x))|\lesssim\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x+\alpha)\right),

|(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))-(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))|\lesssim\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right),

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{(\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x+\alpha))^{2})^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|2f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-f(x+\alpha)||f(x+\alpha)-f(x-\alpha)|}{\alpha^{6}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x)M[\partial_{x}f](x)}{|\alpha|^{3}},

we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2\alpha(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))(\partial_{x}f(x)-\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}-\frac{2\alpha(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))(\partial_{x}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}f(x))}{(\alpha^{2}+(f(x+\alpha)-f(x))^{2})^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x)(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha))+M[\partial_{x}f](x)(M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x-\alpha))

\displaystyle+M[\partial_{x}f](x)^{2}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x)(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)).

Hence

(3.64)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|\alpha|<1}\frac{2\alpha(f(x)-f(x-\alpha))(\partial_{x}f(x)-\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(\Delta f(x,x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x)\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\int_{0<\alpha<1}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x+\alpha)\right)d\alpha.

By Young’s inequality again, we have

\left\|\int_{0<\alpha<1}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x+\alpha)\right)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\mathrm{1}_{0<\alpha<1}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{p+2}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

The second term in (
3.62
) is the sum of four terms:

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha)+\partial_{x}\theta(x))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(g(x)-g(x-\alpha))(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle+(2\sigma+\theta(x))P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle+\partial_{x}\theta(x)P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(g(x)-g(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle+(2\sigma+\theta(x))\partial_{x}\theta(x)P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha.

The estimate of the first term can be obtained in the same way as in (
3.63
)(
3.64
) except that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x+\alpha))^{2})^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|4\sigma+2\theta(x)+2g(x)-g(x-\alpha)-g(x+\alpha)||g(x+\alpha)-g(x-\alpha)|}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{(2\sigma)|\alpha|}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}M[\partial_{x}g](x)+\frac{|\alpha|^{3}M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x)M[\partial_{x}g](x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}.

The additional term
\frac{(2\sigma)\alpha M[\partial_{x}g](x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}
gives rise to

\int_{\mathbb{R}}M[\partial_{x}g](x)^{2}M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x)\frac{(2\sigma)\alpha^{4}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}d\alpha\lesssim M[\partial_{x}g](x)^{2}M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x).

Consequently,

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(g(x)-g(x-\alpha))(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}(1+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2})+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

The second and third terms are controlled by

\displaystyle\left\|(2\sigma+\theta(x))P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\left\|M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{2}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}},

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}\theta(x)P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(g(x)-g(x-\alpha))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}\theta(x)M[\partial_{x}g](x)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{2}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}.

For the last term, we take advantage of the cancellation between the part
\alpha<0
and
\alpha>0
:

\displaystyle\left|(2\sigma+\theta(x))\partial_{x}\theta(x)P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|(2\sigma+\theta(x))\partial_{x}\theta(x)|\left|\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{2\alpha}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}-\frac{2\alpha}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x+\alpha))^{2}}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma|\partial_{x}\theta(x)|\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha|4\sigma+2\theta(x)+2g(x)-g(x-\alpha)-g(x+\alpha)||g(x+\alpha)-g(x-\alpha)|}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma|\partial_{x}\theta(x)|M[\partial_{x}g](x)\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{\alpha^{2}(2\sigma+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\alpha)}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}\theta(x)|M[\partial_{x}g](x).

Hence the
L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}
norm of this term is bounded by
C(2\sigma)^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}
.
Collecting the above bounds yields

\displaystyle\left\|P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{2\alpha(\Delta g(x,x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))(\Delta\partial_{x}g(x,x-\alpha)+\partial_{x}\theta(x))}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+f(x)-g(x-\alpha))^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Then we conclude in view of the condition (
3.17
) and the embedding
L^{p}\subset H^{1}
that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{+}\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{p}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

By repeating all the above arguments, the same bound also holds for
\partial_{x}u_{-}
.
∎

For simplicity, let
B_{1}(t)=1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}
and
B_{2}(t)=\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}
so that

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{12}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Lambda u\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{12}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{12}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+B_{1}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{12}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{12}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{11}{12}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{12}}

\displaystyle+B_{1}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{12}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}},

where we used the inequalities

\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{12}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{5}{6}}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{6}},\quad\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

By Young’s inequality and using
\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{3}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{1}{3}}
, we obtain

(3.65)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[B_{1}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{12}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+B_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{11}{12}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{12}}\right]\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{\frac{4}{3}}

\displaystyle+\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+B_{4}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2},

where
B_{3}:=B_{1}+B_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}
, and

\displaystyle B_{4}:=

\displaystyle B_{1}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle+B_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}.

Likewise,

(3.66)

\displaystyle\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}(u\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim B_{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+B_{4}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

Collecting the bounds in (
3.65
-
3.66
) we conclude with the control for all the transport terms in (
3.1
)(
3.2
):

Lemma 3.12
.

Let
B_{i}
,
1\leq i\leq 4
be given by

(3.67)

\displaystyle B_{1}(t):=

\displaystyle 1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}},\;B_{2}(t):=\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}},

\displaystyle B_{3}(t):=

\displaystyle B_{1}+B_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle B_{4}(t):=

\displaystyle B_{1}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle+B_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}.

Then the transport terms can be controlled by

(3.68)

\displaystyle\left|-\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(\mu_{2}u_{+}+\mu_{1}u_{-})\partial_{x}^{k+1}h\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(\mu_{1}u_{+}+\mu_{2}u_{-})\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}\,dx\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(u_{+}-u_{-})(\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}+\partial_{x}^{k+1}h\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta})dx\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+B_{4}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right).

3.6.
Commutators-Safe terms

The following two sections are devoted to control the commutators. Actually, we will split the commutators into safe terms and easy terms based on the number of derivatives. In this section, we will handle the safe terms, and the main result is given in Lemma
3.14
. To begin with, we observe that the sum of
N_{1}^{h}
and
N_{3}^{h}
has a nice structure:

\displaystyle(\Delta\rho)^{-1}(N^{h}_{1}+N^{h}_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},(\mu_{2}P_{11}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}P_{12}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}P_{21}+\mu_{1}^{2}P_{22})(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}h(x)-\partial_{x}h(x-\alpha))d\alpha,

Meanwhile, the sum of
N_{2}^{h}
and
N_{4}^{h}
can be written as

\displaystyle(\Delta\rho)^{-1}(N_{2}^{h}+N_{4}^{h})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},(\mu_{2}(P_{11}-P_{12})-\mu_{1}(P_{22}-P_{21}))(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x-\alpha))d\alpha

\displaystyle+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},(P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha.

As an analogue, combining
N_{1}^{\theta}
with
N_{3}^{\theta}
and combining
N_{2}^{\theta}
with
N_{4}^{\theta}
yields

\displaystyle(\Delta\rho)^{-1}(N_{1}^{\theta}+N_{3}^{\theta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},(\mu_{2}(P_{11}-P_{12})-\mu_{1}(P_{22}-P_{21}))(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}h(x)-\partial_{x}h(x-\alpha))d\alpha,

\displaystyle(\Delta\rho)^{-1}(N_{2}^{\theta}+N_{4}^{\theta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},(P_{11}+P_{22}-P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}\theta(x)-\partial_{x}\theta(x-\alpha))d\alpha

\displaystyle+\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},(\mu_{2}P_{21}+\mu_{1}P_{12})(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha.

Therefore, we see that the commutators are linear combinations of the following elements:

P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{ij}(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}h(x)-\partial_{x}h(x-\alpha))d\alpha,\;(i,j)\in\left\{1,2\right\}^{2},

P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{ij}(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}\theta(x)-\partial_{x}\theta(x-\alpha))d\alpha,\;(i,j)\in\left\{1,2\right\}^{2},

P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{12}(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha,\;P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{21}(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha.

For convenience, let
q(x):=\frac{1}{1+x^{2}}
and
\tilde{\Delta}w(x,\alpha)=w(x)-w(x-\alpha)
for any function
w
, so that
\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f)^{2}}=\frac{1}{\alpha}q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right).
Then we expand the commutator by Faà di Bruno’s formula,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{11}(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}h(x)-\partial_{x}h(x-\alpha))d\alpha,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq l\leq j\leq k}\sum_{S_{j,l}}A^{k,j,l}_{r_{1},\cdots,r_{j}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\,d\alpha

where
A^{k,j,l}_{r_{1},\cdots,r_{j}}
are harmless constants, and the index set
S_{j,l}
is defined by

S_{j,l}:=\left\{(r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{j})\in\mathbb{Z}^{j}\mid\sum_{1\leq a\leq j}ar_{a}=j,\,\sum_{1\leq a\leq j}r_{a}=l,\,r_{a}\geq 0\right\}.

Likewise, the same identities hold if we replace
h
by
\theta
, or replace
P_{11}
by
P_{22}
and replace
f
by
g
.
To express
\partial_{x}^{j}(P_{12}(x,x-\alpha))
and
\partial_{x}^{j}(P_{21}(x,x-\alpha))
, we write

P_{12}(x,x-\alpha)=\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+\theta(x)+\tilde{\Delta}g)^{2}}=\alpha^{-1}q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right),

P_{21}(x,x-\alpha)=\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{2}}=\alpha^{-1}q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right),

so that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{12}(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}h(x)-\partial_{x}h(x-\alpha))d\alpha,

\displaystyle\ =

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq l\leq j\leq k}\sum_{S_{j,l}}A^{k,j,l}_{r_{1},\cdots,r_{j}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\,d\alpha,

and the same identities hold if we replace
h
by
\theta
, or replace
P_{12}
by
P_{21}
and replace
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
. The rest two terms can be expressed in the same manner:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{12}(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha,

\displaystyle\ =

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq l\leq j\leq k}\sum_{S_{j,l}}A^{k,j,l}_{r_{1},\cdots,r_{j}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k},P_{21}(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha,

\displaystyle\ =

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq l\leq j\leq k}\sum_{S_{j,l}}A^{k,j,l}_{r_{1},\cdots,r_{j}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha.

To sum up, in order to obtain controls over the commutators, it suffices to consider the following three classes of terms.

(i)

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\,d\alpha
for
r:=(r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{j})\in S_{j,l}
,
1\leq l\leq j\leq k
, and the terms with
f
replaced
g
or
h
replaced by
\theta
.

(ii)

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\,d\alpha
for
r:=(r_{1},r_{2},\cdots,r_{j})\in S_{j,l}
,
1\leq l\leq j\leq k
and the terms with
h
replaced by
\theta
or
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
and its derivatives replaced by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
and its derivatives, respectively.

(iii)

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha
for
1\leq l\leq j\leq k
,
r\in S_{j,l}
and the terms with
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
and its derivatives replaced by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
and its derivatives, respectively.

Moreover, we distinguish the end point terms from each class, which are the terms involving the derivatives of
k^{\text{th}}
order of
h
,
\theta
,
f
or
g
. In the following, we will refer to these terms as safe terms and refer to the other terms as easy terms. To be specific, we list all the safe terms from each class in the following.

•

Safe terms in class (i):

When
l=j=1
,
r=r_{1}=1
, we get

Safe^{11}_{h,1}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha,\;Safe^{22}_{h,1}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha,

and
Safe^{11}_{\theta,1}
,
Safe^{22}_{\theta,1}
, which are defined by replacing
h
by
\theta
in the above expressions.

When
j=k
,
l=1
,
r=(0,0,\cdots,1)
, we get

Safe^{11}_{h,2}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}f}{\alpha}d\alpha,\;Safe^{22}_{h,2}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}g}{\alpha}d\alpha,

and
Safe^{11}_{\theta,2}
,
Safe^{22}_{\theta,2}
, which are defined by replacing
h
by
\theta
in the above expressions.

•

Safe terms in class (ii):

When
l=j=1
,
r=r_{1}=1
