, we get

\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g+\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha=Safe_{h,1}^{12}(x)+Safe_{h,2}^{12}(x),

where

Safe_{h,1}^{12}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha,

Safe_{h,2}^{12}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha.

Replacing
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
yields

Safe_{h,1}^{21}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha,

Safe_{h,2}^{21}(x):=-\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha.

When
j=k
,
l=1
,
r=(0,0,\cdots,1)
, we get

\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}g+\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha=Safe_{h,3}^{12}(x)+Safe_{h,4}^{12}(x),

where

Safe_{h,3}^{12}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}g}{\alpha}d\alpha,

Safe_{h,4}^{12}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha.

Replacing
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
gives

Safe_{h,3}^{21}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}f}{\alpha}d\alpha,

Safe_{h,4}^{21}(x):=-\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha.

We also define
Safe_{\theta,1}^{12}
,
Safe_{\theta,2}^{12}
,
Safe_{\theta,1}^{21}
,
Safe_{\theta,2}^{21}
by replacing
\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}
by
\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta}{\alpha}
in the expressions of
Safe_{h,1}^{12}
,
Safe_{h,2}^{12}
,
Safe_{h,1}^{21}
,
Safe_{h,2}^{21}
, and define
Safe_{\theta,3}^{12}
,
Safe_{\theta,4}^{12}
,
Safe_{\theta,3}^{21}
,
Safe_{\theta,4}^{21}
by replacing
\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}
by
\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}\theta}{\alpha}
in the expressions of
Safe_{h,3}^{12}
,
Safe_{h,4}^{12}
,
Safe_{h,3}^{21}
,
Safe_{h,4}^{21}
, respectively. Hence we have in total 16 safe terms in class (ii).

•

Safe terms in class (iii):
When
l=j=1
,
r=r_{1}=1
, we get

\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g+\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha=Safe_{\theta,5}^{12}(x)+Safe_{\theta,6}^{12}(x),

where

Safe_{\theta,5}^{12}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha,

Safe_{\theta,6}^{12}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha.

When
j=k
,
l=1
,
r=(0,0,\cdots,1)
, we get

\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}g+\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha=Safe_{\theta,7}^{12}(x)+Safe_{\theta,6}^{12}(x),

where

Safe_{\theta,7}^{12}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}g}{\alpha}d\alpha.

Then we replace
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
and obtain
Safe^{21}_{\theta,5}
,
Safe^{21}_{\theta,6}
,
Safe^{21}_{\theta,7}
.

Now we derive the controls over each of the above safe terms.

Controls for class (i)
. For
Safe_{h,1}^{11}
, we decompose it into three terms:

\displaystyle Safe_{h,1}^{11}(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha-q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}\left(\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)\right)d\alpha.

For the first term, we use
|q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))|\lesssim|\partial_{x}f(x)|
and

\displaystyle\left|P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha+\int_{0<\alpha\leq 1}\frac{2\partial_{x}f(x)-\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha)-\partial_{x}f(x+\alpha)}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|>1}\alpha^{-1}M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)d\alpha+\int_{0<\alpha\leq 1}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{3}f](x+\alpha)\right)d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

to obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma}(t)}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}(t)}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}(t)}.

For the second term, we notice that
\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)d\alpha
is the first Calderón commutator
[
33
, Page 254]
applied on
\partial_{x}^{k}h
. Since the first Calderón commutator is
L^{2}
bounded with norm
C\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}}
, we have

\left\|q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

The third term is a regular integral. Notice that

\left|\alpha^{-1}\tilde{\Delta}f-\partial_{x}f(x)\right|\lesssim|\alpha|\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma}(t)}\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|\leq 1}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma}(t)}\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|>1},

\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha}\right|\leq\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|\leq 1}+\alpha^{-1}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|>1}.

Hence we have

\left|\left(q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}\right|\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|\leq 1}+\alpha^{-1}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|>1}\right)^{2}.

Since the right-hand side of the above inequality is a
L^{1}
smooth function of
\alpha
, and its
L^{1}
norm is bounded by
C\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{2}
, using Young’s inequality yields

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(1)}(\partial_{x}f(x))\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}f}{\alpha^{2}}\left(\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)\right)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Collecting the controls for the three terms above, we obtain

\displaystyle\|Safe_{h,1}^{11}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right]\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Likewise, the other safe terms in class (i) can be controlled in the same way. To sum up, for
w=h
or
w=\theta

(3.69)

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\{1,2\}}\left(\|Safe_{w,i}^{11}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{w,i}^{22}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}w\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\|\partial_{x}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}w\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right].

Controls for class (ii)
. First, we decompose
q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)
into odd and even parts:

\displaystyle q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)=q^{(1)}_{odd}(x,x-\alpha)+q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha),

where

\displaystyle q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)+\frac{1}{2}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha^{3}(\tilde{\Delta}f+\tilde{\Delta}g)}{2}\left[\left((\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}+\left((\tilde{\Delta}g-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha^{3}(4\sigma+\theta(x)+\theta(x-\alpha))}{2}\left[\left((\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}-\left((\tilde{\Delta}g-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}\right],

\displaystyle q^{(1)}_{odd}(x,x-\alpha)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)-\frac{1}{2}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha^{3}(\tilde{\Delta}f+\tilde{\Delta}g)}{2}\left[\left((\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}-\left((\tilde{\Delta}g-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha^{3}(4\sigma+\theta(x)+\theta(x-\alpha))}{2}\left[\left((\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}+\left((\tilde{\Delta}g-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}\right].

Let
K_{g}^{12}(x,x-\alpha):=q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g}{\alpha^{2}}
,
L_{g}^{12}(x,x-\alpha):=q^{(1)}_{odd}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g}{\alpha^{2}},
and define the operator
(T_{g}^{12}h)(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}K_{g}^{12}(x,x-\alpha)h(x-\alpha)d\alpha.
To derive the control for
Safe_{h,1}^{12}
, we apply the
T1
theorem
[
33
, Page 236-253]
upon
T_{g}^{12}
. To this end, we check in the following lemma that the operator
T_{g}^{12}
and its kernel
K_{g}^{12}
satisfy the conditions required by the
T1
theorem.

Lemma 3.13
.

Suppose the solution satisfies the condition

(3.70)

\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\delta_{1}

for a universal small constant
\delta_{1}>0
. Then
K_{g}^{12}
is a standard antisymmetric kernel, and
T_{g}^{12}(1)
is
L^{\infty}
.

Proof.

By the construction of
q^{(1)}_{even}
, we immediately have
q^{(1)}_{even}(x,y)=q^{(1)}_{even}(y,x)
, and thus
K_{g}^{12}(x,y)=-K_{g}^{12}(y,x)
, i.e.
K_{g}^{12}
is antisymmetric. For the size condition, by an elementary computation using condition (
3.70
), there is

\displaystyle\left|\left((\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}-\left((\tilde{\Delta}g-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha))^{2}+\alpha^{2}\right)^{-2}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{-2},

so that

(3.71)

|q^{(1)}_{even}(x,y)|\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\frac{|x-y|^{4}}{(|x-y|^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}},

\displaystyle|K_{g}^{12}(x,y)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}|x-y|^{-1}.

For the regularity condition, suppose now
|y_{1}-y_{2}|\leq\frac{1}{2}\max\left\{|x-y_{1}|,|x-y_{2}|\right\}
and consequently
|x-y_{1}|\simeq|x-y_{2}|
. Through a lengthy but elementary computation with the help of Lemma
3.5
, it can be shown that

\displaystyle|q^{(1)}_{even}(x,y_{1})-q^{(1)}_{even}(x,y_{2})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot|y_{1}-y_{2}|\left(|x-y_{1}|^{3}+|x-y_{2}|^{3}\right)\left[(|x-y_{1}|^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{-2}+(|x-y_{2}|^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{-2}\right].

Meanwhile, we have

|\partial_{x}g(y_{1})-\partial_{x}g(y_{2})|\leq\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}}|y_{1}-y_{2}|
and
|x-y_{1}|^{-1}-|x-y_{2}|^{-1}\leq\frac{|y_{1}-y_{2}|}{|x-y_{1}||x-y_{2}|}
. By Lemma
3.5
again, we conclude that

|K_{g}^{12}(x,y_{1})-K_{g}^{12}(x,y_{2})|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)|y_{1}-y_{2}|(|x-y_{1}|^{-2}+|x-y_{2}|^{-2}).

Notice that
K_{g}^{12}(x,y)
is well-defined on the diagonal
x=y
since
q^{(1)}_{even}
provides the factor
\alpha^{3}
. We write

\displaystyle(T_{g}^{12}1)(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\alpha>0}\alpha^{-2}\left(q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha))+q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x+\alpha)(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x+\alpha))\right)d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\alpha>0}q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{2\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha)-\partial_{x}g(x+\alpha)}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x+\alpha)-q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x+\alpha)}{\alpha}d\alpha.

For the first term, we use (
3.71
) and

\displaystyle\left|\alpha^{-2}(2\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha)-\partial_{x}g(x+\alpha))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(M[\partial_{x}^{3}g](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{3}g(x+\alpha)]\right)\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|\leq 1}+\alpha^{-1}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)\right)\mathrm{1}_{|\alpha|>1}

to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\alpha>0}q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{2\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x-\alpha)-\partial_{x}g(x+\alpha)}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

For the second term, a direct computation with the help of Lemma
3.5
gives

\displaystyle\left|(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x))-(g(x+\alpha)-g(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x+\alpha))\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left|\left(2f(x)+2g(x)-f(x-\alpha)-g(x-\alpha)-f(x+\alpha)-g(x+\alpha)\right)+(\theta(x-\alpha)-\theta(x+\alpha))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x-\alpha)\right)+|\alpha|M[\partial_{x}\theta](x+\alpha).

and likewise,

\displaystyle\left|(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x-\alpha))-(g(x+\alpha)-g(x)-2\sigma-\theta(x))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x-\alpha)\right)+|\alpha|M[\partial_{x}\theta](x+\alpha).

Then it follows by Lemma
3.5
that

\displaystyle\left|\alpha^{-1}\left(q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x+\alpha)-q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\left[\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\frac{\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}M[\partial_{x}\theta](x+\alpha)\right]+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x-\alpha)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}})\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f+\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)\right)

\displaystyle+(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}})\sigma^{-1}M[\partial_{x}\theta](x+\alpha).

Together with
\left|\alpha^{-1}(\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x+\alpha))\right|\leq M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\alpha>0}\frac{q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x+\alpha)-q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}g(x)-\partial_{x}g(x+\alpha)}{\alpha}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Collecting the above bounds gives

\displaystyle|T_{g}^{12}1|\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right]\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

∎

Now applying the
T1
theorem upon
T_{g}^{12}
yields

\displaystyle\|T_{g}^{12}\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Meanwhile, since
T_{g}^{12}1
is
L^{\infty}
, we also have

\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}K_{g}^{12}(x,x-\alpha)\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|T_{g}^{12}1\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

and it follows

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}g}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

In order to control
Safe_{h,1}^{12}
, it remains to bound
\int_{\mathbb{R}}L^{12}_{g}(x,x-\alpha)(\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha))d\alpha
. By observing that

(3.72)

|q^{(1)}_{odd}(x,x-\alpha)|\lesssim\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\frac{|\alpha^{3}|\sigma}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}},

Young’s inequality then gives

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}L^{12}_{g}(x,x-\alpha)\left(\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)\right)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{2}\sigma}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}\left(|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)|+|\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)|\right)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Therefore, under the condition (
3.70
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\|Safe_{h,1}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Since
Safe_{w,i}^{12}
,
Safe_{w,i}^{21}
,
w\in\{h,\theta\}
,
i\in\{1,3\}
share the same structure, repeating the above arguments yields for
w=h,\theta
that

(3.73)

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\{1,3\}}\left(\|Safe_{w,i}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe^{21}_{w,i}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}w\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}w\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Next, we are devoted to the control over
Safe_{h,4}^{12}
. Introduce the decomposition

q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha^{2}}=K^{12}_{h}(x,x-\alpha)+L_{h}^{12}(x,x-\alpha),

K^{12}_{h}(x,x-\alpha)=q^{(1)}_{even}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha^{2}},\quad L^{12}_{h}(x,x-\alpha)=q^{(1)}_{odd}(x,x-\alpha)\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha^{2}}.

By mimicking the estimate of
T_{g}^{12}1
as in the proof of Lemma
3.13
, it can be shown that

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}K^{12}_{h}(x,x-\alpha)d\alpha\right|\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Meanwhile, using (
3.72
) again, we have

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}L^{12}_{h}(x,x-\alpha)d\alpha\right|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{2}\sigma}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}d\alpha\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Taking the sum yields
\|Safe_{h,4}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.
In the same manner, we can obtain the controls over
Safe_{h,4}^{21}
,
Safe_{\theta,4}^{12}
and
Safe_{\theta,4}^{21}
. For
w=h,\theta
,

(3.74)

\displaystyle\|Safe_{w,4}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{w,4}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}w\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

We decompose
Safe_{h,2}^{12}(x)
as

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha\tilde{\Delta}g\cdot\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})^{2}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h\,d\alpha-2\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})^{2}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h\,d\alpha.

The first term can be controlled easily by

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha\tilde{\Delta}g\cdot\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})^{2}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h\,d\alpha\right|\lesssim\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

To control the second term, let
\varphi\in L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
be arbitrary. By Hölder inequality, it holds

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})^{2}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h\,d\alpha\right)\varphi(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{4}\sigma^{2}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{3}}|\varphi(x)|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Hence

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))\partial_{x}\theta(x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})^{2}}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h}{\alpha}\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Recall from Proposition
3.6
that
D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\simeq\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}
. Summing the above two bounds yields

\displaystyle\|Safe_{h,2}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D^{0}_{22}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

The controls over
Safe_{h,2}^{21}
,
Safe_{\theta,2}^{12}
and
Safe_{\theta,2}^{21}
can be obtained in the same way. To sum up, we have for
w=h,\theta

(3.75)

\displaystyle\|Safe_{w,2}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{w,2}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}w\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D^{0}_{22}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}w|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Controls for class (iii)
.
Note that
Safe_{\theta,5}^{12}
has the same structure as
Safe_{h,4}^{12}
, and
Safe_{\theta,7}^{12}
has the same structure as
Safe_{h,2}^{12}
. Hence by repeating the corresponding arguments, similar estimates can be obtained for
Safe_{\theta,5}^{12}
,
Safe_{\theta,7}^{12}
. Moreover, by replacing
g
by
f
, we also have the estimates of
Safe_{\theta,5}^{21}
,
Safe_{\theta,7}^{21}
.

(3.76)

\displaystyle\|Safe_{\theta,5}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{\theta,5}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

(3.77)

\displaystyle\|Safe_{\theta,7}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{\theta,7}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}f|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}g|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

In order to control
Safe_{\theta,6}^{12}
and
Safe_{\theta,6}^{21}
, it suffices to bound
\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\alpha^{-2}d\alpha
. Since
q^{(1)}
is an odd function, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\alpha^{-2}d\alpha=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(1)}\left(\frac{2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]\alpha^{-2}d\alpha.

In view of
\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq w_{0}\lesssim 1
, it holds

\displaystyle\left|q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(1)}\left(\frac{2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{|\tilde{\Delta}g||\alpha^{3}|}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))\right)^{2}}+2|2\sigma+\theta(x)||\alpha^{3}|\left|\frac{1}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))\right)^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\frac{\alpha^{4}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{2}}.

Hence
\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\alpha^{-2}d\alpha\right|\lesssim\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}
, and it follows that

\|Safe_{\theta,6}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

The same bound with
g
replaced by
f
holds for
\|Safe_{\theta,6}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}
. That is

(3.78)

\|Safe_{\theta,6}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{\theta,6}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Recall that
f=h+\mu_{1}\theta
,
g=h-\mu_{2}\theta
, and
\|\partial_{x}^{j}f\|_{X}+\|\partial_{x}^{j}g\|_{X}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{j}h\|_{X}+\|\partial_{x}^{j}\theta\|_{X}
for all
j\geq 0
and
X=L^{2}_{\gamma(t)},H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}
or
L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}
. We collect (
3.69
) and (
3.73
-
3.78
) to conclude with the lemma below.

Lemma 3.14
.

Suppose that the solution satisfies conditions (
3.17
) and (
3.70
). Then all the safe terms satisfy the bound

\displaystyle\sum_{w\in\{h,\theta\}}\sum_{i\in\{1,2\}}\left(\|Safe_{w,i}^{11}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{w,i}^{22}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\sum_{5\leq i\leq 7}\left(\|Safe_{\theta,i}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{\theta,i}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{w\in\{h,\theta\}}\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}\left(\|Safe_{w,i}^{12}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|Safe_{w,i}^{21}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\sum_{w\in\{h,\theta\}}\|\partial_{x}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}w\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(,l,rt)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

3.7.
Commutators-Easy terms

In the next, we are devoted to the controls over the easy terms, which correspond to the case
k-1\geq j\geq 2
or
j=k
,
l\geq 2
. For convenience, denote the set of such index
(j,l)
by
B_{k}:=\left\{(j,l)\mid 2\leq j\leq k,\,1\leq l\leq j,\,(j,l)\neq(k,1)\right\}.

Controls for class (i)
.
First, in view of condition (
3.17
) and the fact that
q^{(l)}
,
l\geq 0
are smooth functions, we always have
\left|q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\right|+\left|q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g}{\alpha}\right)\right|\lesssim 1
. Let
\tilde{k}:=\left\lfloor\frac{k+1}{2}\right\rfloor
. If
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}
, we write

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\left|\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\right|d\alpha\cdot M[\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h](x)+\int_{|\alpha|>1}\left|\alpha^{-1}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h)\right|d\alpha.

By virtue of
\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right|\leq\|\partial_{x}^{a+1}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}
, it holds

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\left|\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\right|d\alpha\lesssim\prod_{a=1}^{j}\|\partial_{x}^{a+1}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{r_{a}}.

Since
r_{a}=0
for
a>\tilde{k}
and
\sum_{a=1}^{j}r_{a}=l
, we have by Sobolev embedding that

\prod_{a=1}^{j}\|\partial_{x}^{a+1}f\|^{r_{a}}_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l}.

Hence the
L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
norm of the part
|\alpha|\leq 1
can be controlled by
\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}
.
For the part
|\alpha|>1
, we have

\left|\alpha^{-1}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\right|\lesssim|\alpha|^{-1-l}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\|\partial_{x}^{a}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{r_{a}}\lesssim|\alpha|^{-1-l}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l}.

Hence by Minkowski

\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\left|\alpha^{-1}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h)\right|d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l}\|\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Since
2\leq k+2-j\leq k
, we remark that
\|\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Now suppose otherwise that there exists
a_{0}>\tilde{k}
such that
r_{a_{0}}\neq 0
. Recall that
\sum_{a=1}^{j}ar_{a}=j\leq k
and
(j,l)\neq(k,1)
. Hence we must have
a_{0}+1\leq k
,
j>\tilde{k}
,
r_{a_{0}}=1
and
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}
with
a\neq a_{0}
. Moreover,
a_{0}\geq 5
since we choose
k\geq 10
. In this case, we write

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|>1}\left|\alpha^{-1}\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}f)\right|d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\left|\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha\cdot M[\partial_{x}^{a_{0}+1}f](x).

Since
|\alpha^{-1}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h|\leq\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}
and
\tilde{k}+1\geq k+2-j\geq 2
, we have

\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\left|\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Hence the
L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
norm of the part
|\alpha|\leq 1
can be controlled by
\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}+1}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.
For the part
|\alpha|>1
, there is

\displaystyle\left|\alpha^{-1}\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{-1-l}\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\|\partial_{x}^{a}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{r_{a}}\|\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{-1-l}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Then using Minkowski and noticing
5\leq a_{0}\leq k-1
yields

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\left|\alpha^{-1}\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}f)\right|d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{5}f\|_{H^{k-6}_{\gamma(t)}}.

We can also obtain similar controls for other easy terms in class (i) with
f
replaced by
g
or
h
replaced by
\theta
. Using that
\|\partial_{x}^{j}f\|_{X}+\|\partial_{x}^{j}g\|_{X}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{j}h\|_{X}+\|\partial_{x}^{j}\theta\|_{X}
for all
j\geq 0
and
X=L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}
or
H^{m}_{\gamma(t)}
,
m\geq 0
, we obtain the following control for easy terms of class (i):

Lemma 3.15
.

Suppose that condition (
3.17
) holds. Then

(3.79)

\displaystyle\sum_{B_{k}}\sum_{S_{j,l}}\sum_{w^{\prime}\in\{f,g\}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}w^{\prime}}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}w^{\prime}}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}w}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\delta_{2})^{k-1}\left[\delta_{2}\|w\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}w\|_{H^{\tilde{{k}}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{5}f\|_{H^{k-6}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{5}g\|_{H^{k-6}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta_{2}(1+\delta_{2})^{k-1}\left(\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

where we denote for simplicity

\delta_{2}(t):=\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}
.

Controls for class (ii)
. To begin with, we observe that

q^{(l)}(x)=\frac{l!}{2}\left(\frac{(-i)^{l}}{(1+ix)^{l+1}}+\frac{i^{l}}{(1-ix)^{l+1}}\right)
. Suppose first that
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}
. In view of condition (
3.17
), for any
a\in[1,\tilde{k}]
, it holds

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right|+\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{a+1}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Then using Lemma
3.5
we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}[\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}]^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{r_{a}\neq 0}|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g|.

Since
|\alpha^{-1}|\geq[\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}]^{-\frac{1}{2}}
and
\left|\frac{\alpha}{\alpha\pm i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right|\lesssim 1
, it follows that

(3.80)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\sum_{r_{a}\neq 0}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha.

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+(-1)^{l}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}\right]\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right|

To control the first term, by repeating the arguments in the controls for class (i), for each
a\leq\tilde{k}
we have

(3.81)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

For the second term, if
l\geq 2
, using Hölder’s inequality with arbitrary
\varphi\in L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
gives

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right)\overline{\varphi(x)}dx\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{r_{a}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{l}}{[\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}]^{\frac{l}{2}}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\right||\varphi(x)|d\alpha dx\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{r_{a}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2l-2}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{l-1}}|\varphi(x)|^{2}dxd\alpha\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{r_{a}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

which, in view of
\|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}
, implies that

(3.82)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Likewise,

(3.83)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

If
l=1
, then
2\leq j\leq\tilde{k}
,
r_{j}=1
and
r_{a}=0
,
1\leq a\leq j-1
.
In this case, we use the identity

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{4i}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\frac{\alpha\partial_{x}^{j}\theta(x)}{\left(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))\right)^{2}}-\frac{\alpha\partial_{x}^{j}\theta(x)}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{2}}\right]\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{2}\tilde{\Delta}g\cdot\partial_{x}^{j}\theta(x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\alpha^{2}\partial_{x}^{j}\theta(x)(2\sigma+\theta(x))}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha.

The right-hand side of the above identity has the same structure as
Safe_{h,2}^{12}
(see (
3.75
)), so by repetition of the proof for (
3.75
), we obtain

(3.84)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\frac{\alpha\partial_{x}^{j}\theta(x)}{\left(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))\right)^{2}}-\frac{\alpha\partial_{x}^{j}\theta(x)}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{2}}\right]\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{j}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{j}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Collecting the inequalities (
3.80
-
3.84
) and noticing that
\|\partial_{x}^{j}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}
for
j\leq\tilde{k}
, we conclude that

(3.85)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left[1+\sigma^{1-l}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\right]\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now suppose otherwise that there exists
a_{0}>\tilde{k}
such that
r_{a_{0}}\neq 0
. Then
j>\tilde{k}
,
a_{0}+1\leq k
,
r_{a_{0}}=1
and
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}
with
a\neq a_{0}
. In this case, we use the decomposition

\displaystyle q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}

\displaystyle+q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}

Note that
\left|q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{|\alpha|^{l+1}}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{\frac{l+1}{2}}}
, and an analogue of (
3.81
) gives

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|g\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Hence we get

(3.86)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

For the other term, we further decompose it into two parts:

\displaystyle q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha^{-l}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)\right)^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g\right)^{r_{a}}\right]\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\cdot\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\cdot\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x),

The first term on the right-hand side disappears if
l=1
. If otherwise
l>1
, using Lemma
3.5
and
\left\|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}
, we get

(3.87)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x))^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g)^{r_{a}}\right]\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{(\alpha\pm i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-2}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}d\alpha\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-2}\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

To control the second term, it suffices to bound the
L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}
norm of

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha.

To this end, we decompose the integral over
\alpha
into the parts
|\alpha|\leq 1
and
|\alpha|>1
. Denote
R_{l}(x,\alpha;g):=\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)
for simplicity. The part
|\alpha|>1
can be controlled easily:

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)d\alpha\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{1}{|\alpha|}M[\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h](x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

where we used the
L^{2}
boundedness of the maximal functional,
2\leq k+2-j\leq 1+\tilde{k}
and that
|R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)|\lesssim|\alpha|^{-1}
. As to the part
|\alpha|\leq 1
, we write

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}-\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}(\partial_{x}^{a+1}g(x))^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h(x)\right]d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)d\alpha\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}(\partial_{x}^{a+1}g(x))^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h(x).

Observe that
|R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)|\lesssim(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}
and that
\left|\alpha^{-1}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g-\partial_{x}^{a+1}g(x)\right|\lesssim|\alpha|M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}g](x-\alpha)
,
\left|\alpha^{-1}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h-\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h(x)\right|\lesssim|\alpha|M[\partial_{x}^{k+3-j}h](x-\alpha)
.
By Lemma
3.5
and noticing
k+3-j\leq\tilde{k}+2
, it holds

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}-\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}(\partial_{x}^{a+1}g(x))^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h(x)\right]d\alpha\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathrm{1}_{l\geq 2}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-2}\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\sum_{a\neq a_{0}}M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}g](x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}M[\partial_{x}^{k+3-j}h](x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|^{l-1}_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Meanwhile, we write

\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)d\alpha=\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\left(R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)+R_{l}(x,-\alpha;g)\right)d\alpha,

and direct computation using condition (
3.17
) shows

\displaystyle\left|R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)+R_{l}(x,-\alpha;g)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{l!}{2}\left|\frac{\alpha^{l}}{(\alpha+i(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}-\frac{\alpha^{l}}{(\alpha-i(g(x)-g(x+\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{(-\alpha)^{l}}{(\alpha-i(g(x)-g(x-\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}-\frac{(-\alpha)^{l}}{(\alpha+i(g(x)-g(x+\alpha)+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}g](x+\alpha)+\frac{\sigma}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}.

Using the above inequality, we obtain

\left|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)d\alpha\right|\lesssim 1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}
, and consequently,

(3.88)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}R_{l}(x,\alpha;g)d\alpha\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}(\partial_{x}^{a+1}g(x))^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h(x)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}},

(3.89)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Collecting (
3.87
-
3.89
) yields

(3.90)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Then using
k+2-j\leq\tilde{k}+1
,
5\leq a_{0}\leq k-1
and summing (
3.86
)(
3.90
) gives

(3.91)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}h}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{5}g\|_{H^{k-5}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{5}\theta\|_{H^{k-5}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Moreover, with
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
as well as its derivatives replaced by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
and its derivatives respectively, or with
h
replaced by
\theta
, similar controls as in (
3.85
) and (
3.91
) can be established for any other easy terms in class (ii). Summing up all these controls and using
f=h+\mu_{1}\theta
,
g=h-\mu_{2}\theta
, we get

Lemma 3.16
.

Suppose that condition (
3.17
) holds. Then for
w=h
or
w=\theta

(3.92)

\displaystyle\sum_{B_{k}}\sum_{S_{j,l}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}w}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{B_{k}}\sum_{S_{j,l}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}w}{\alpha}d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}w\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{5}h\|_{H^{k-5}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{5}\theta\|_{H^{k-5}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|w\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right]

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{j=2}^{k}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}w\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Controls for class (iii)
. As before, we first look at the case
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}.
For any
(j,l)\in B_{k}
,
r\in S_{j,l}
, choose an arbitrary
a_{1}
such that
r_{a_{1}}\neq 0
. Then we use the decomposition

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{1}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{1}}g}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{1}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{1}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha.

Observing that the first term has the same structure as the left-hand side of (
3.90
), by adjusting the proof of (
3.90
), it can be shown that

(3.93)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{1}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{1}}g}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

We further decompose the second term into two parts
M_{1}
and
M_{2}
:

M_{1}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-l-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)d\alpha\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\partial_{x}^{r_{a}}\theta(x)\right)^{r_{a}}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x),

\displaystyle M_{2}:=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{1}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a\neq a_{1}}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{1}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x).

If
l=1
, then
M_{2}
disappears. If otherwise
l\geq 1
, using Lemma
3.5
, we obtain

(3.94)

\displaystyle\|M_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

To control
M_{1}
, it suffices to bound
\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-l-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)d\alpha
. A key observation is that
\alpha^{-1-l}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)
is an odd function of
\alpha
for fixed
x
and all
l\geq 1
. Meanwhile, a direct computation shows

\displaystyle\left|\alpha^{-l-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)-\alpha^{-l-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right|\lesssim|\tilde{\Delta}g|\left(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-\frac{l+2}{2}}.

It follows that
\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-l-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)d\alpha\right|\lesssim\sigma^{-l}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}
, and thus

(3.95)

\displaystyle\|M_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l}\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Summing up (
3.94
)(
3.95
) yields

(3.96)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{1}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{1}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}},

and together with (
3.93
) we conclude

(3.97)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}},

Suppose now that there exists
a_{0}>\tilde{k}
with
r_{a_{0}}\neq 0
so that
r_{a_{0}}=1
,
a_{0}+1\leq k
,
j>\tilde{k}
and
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}
with
a\neq a_{0}
. In this case, we write

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}\,d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha

Note that the second term has the same structure as the left-hand side of (
3.96
). Hence we have

(3.98)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

We further decompose the first term into two parts
M_{3}
and
M_{4}
:

M_{3}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}d\alpha,

\displaystyle M_{4}:=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}\,d\alpha.

If
l=1
,
M_{4}
disappears. If otherwise
l\geq 2
, using Lemma
3.5
, Minkowski and the fact
\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|g\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}
gives

(3.99)

\displaystyle\|M_{4}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g|d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|g\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

M_{3}
can be estimated in the same way as in (
3.82
)(
3.83
) if
l\geq 2
or (
3.84
) if
l=1
. To be specific, we have in both cases

(3.100)

\displaystyle\|M_{3}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left[1+\sigma^{1-l}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\right]\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|g\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Collecting (
3.98
-
3.100
), we conclude that

(3.101)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|g\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left[1+\sigma^{1-l}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\right]\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g\right|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

With
\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)
and its derivatives replaced by
\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)
and its derivatives, respectively, similar controls as in (
3.97
) or (
3.101
) for other easy terms in class (iii) can be established. In view of
f=h+\mu_{1}\theta
and
g=h-\mu_{2}\theta
, we finally obtain

Lemma 3.17
.

Suppose that the solution satisfies conditions (
3.17
). Then all the easy terms of class (iii) satisfy the bound

(3.102)

\displaystyle\sum_{B_{k}}\sum_{S_{j,l}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{B_{k}}\sum_{S_{j,l}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k}\left(\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{a_{0}=\tilde{k}+1}^{k-1}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)\left(\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}h\right|^{2}+\left|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta\right|^{2}\right)d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

3.8.
Energy estimate uniform in
\sigma

We hereby collect all the estimates obtained in section
3
to conclude with the a priori energy estimate for the solution. For simplicity, we introduce the notations

\text{Diss}_{k}:=\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D^{0}_{11}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}dxdx_{1}+\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

B_{5}(t):=\left(1+\delta_{2}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k},

and recall the notation
B_{i}
in (
3.67
).
By symmetry, it holds

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h(x,x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|^{2}dxd\alpha=

\displaystyle 2\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}h(x-\alpha)}{\alpha^{2}}d\alpha\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h(x)}\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\pi\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Lambda\partial_{x}^{k}h\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}h}\,dx.

Hence
\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{11}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}dxdx_{1}\simeq\|h\|_{\dot{H}^{k+\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}},
and consequently

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{k}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}D_{11}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{j}h|^{2}dxdx_{1}\lesssim\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}D_{11}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\left(|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}h|^{2}+|\Delta h|^{2}\right)dxdx_{1}.

As to the dissipation in
\theta
, we write

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x,x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|^{2}dxd\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}P.V.\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}(\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)-\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x-\alpha))d\alpha\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2\sigma}\arctan\frac{2\sigma}{\alpha}\right)\partial_{x}^{k+1}\theta(x-\alpha)d\alpha\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x)}\,dx.

The Fourier transform of
