\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2\sigma}\arctan\frac{2\sigma}{\alpha}
can be obtained through a simple computation:

\mathcal{F}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2\sigma}\arctan\frac{2\sigma}{\alpha}\right)(\xi)=-i\pi\text{sign}(\xi)\left(1-\frac{1-e^{-2\sigma|\xi|}}{2\sigma|\xi|}\right).

Therefore, Plancheral identity yields

(3.103)

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x,x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|^{2}dxd\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\pi\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\xi|^{2k}\left(|\xi|-\frac{1-e^{-2\sigma|\xi|}}{2\sigma}\right)|\hat{\theta}(\xi)|^{2}\cosh\left(2\gamma(t)\xi\right)d\xi.

The above inequality shows

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{k}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{j}\theta|^{2}dxdx_{1}\lesssim\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\left(|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta|^{2}+|\Delta\theta|^{2}\right)dxdx_{1},

and the claim is proved. We also remark that (
3.103
) indicates that we do not gain any derivatives of
\theta
uniformly in
\sigma
from the dissipation. Actually, if we suppose
|\xi|\leq\sigma^{-\frac{1}{3}}
for example, then

|\xi|-\frac{1-e^{-2\sigma|\xi|}}{2\sigma}\leq\sigma|\xi|^{2}\leq\sigma^{\frac{1}{3}}.

Therefore, as
\sigma
approaches
0
,
|\xi|-\frac{1-e^{-2\sigma|\xi|}}{2\sigma}
goes to
0
for
\xi
in the region
\left[-\sigma^{-\frac{1}{3}},\sigma^{-\frac{1}{3}}\right]
, which tends to
\mathbb{R}
.

Now, gathering the controls given by Lemma
3.2
,
3.8
,
3.10
,
3.12
,
3.14
and Lemma
3.15
-
3.17
in the equation resulted from
multiplying equation (
3.1
) by
\overline{\partial^{k}_{x}h}
, multiplying equation (
3.2
) by
\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\overline{\partial^{k}_{x}\theta}
, and integrating the real part of the sum along
x\in\Gamma_{\pm}(t)
yields the following inequality as long as conditions (
3.17
) and (
3.70
) hold.

(3.104)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+c_{0}\text{Diss}_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(B_{5}+w_{0})\left(\delta_{2}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{k}\text{Diss}_{j}

\displaystyle+\left(B_{4}+B_{5}\delta_{2}-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\right)\left(\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+B_{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left[\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

where
c_{0}
is a universal constant. Meanwhile, if we set
k=0
so that there is no commutator, then

(3.105)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|h\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+c_{0}\text{Diss}_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[w_{0}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\text{Diss}_{0}

\displaystyle+\left[B_{4}+\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)-2\gamma^{\prime}\tanh(2\gamma(t))\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)+B_{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left[\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Use Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and (
3.103
) for the first terms on the right-hand sides of (
3.104
) and (
3.105
) to get

(3.106)

\displaystyle(B_{5}+w_{0})\left(\delta_{2}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{k}\text{Diss}_{j}

\displaystyle+\left[w_{0}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\text{Diss}_{0}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cb_{1}^{-1}(B_{5}+w_{0})^{2}\left(\delta_{2}^{2}+\sigma+\|\partial_{x}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+b_{1}\left(\text{Diss}_{0}^{2}+\text{Diss}_{k}^{2}\right).

Next, using the pointwise interpolation inequality
\|\partial^{2}_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}
(see
[
37
, Theorem 1]
) and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality for the last terms on the right-hand sides of (
3.104
)(
3.105
) yields

\displaystyle\left[\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left[\|\partial_{x}^{3}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-2}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right).

Summing (
3.104
)(
3.105
) and choosing
b_{1}
small enough shows

(3.107)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\frac{c_{0}}{2}\left(\text{Diss}_{0}^{2}+\text{Diss}_{k}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\left(2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))+C_{3}B_{7}\right)\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(B_{6}-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\right)\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

where
C_{3}
denotes the implicit constant and

\displaystyle B_{6}(t):=

\displaystyle(B_{5}+w_{0})^{2}\left(\delta_{2}^{2}+\sigma+\|\partial_{x}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+B_{4}+B_{5}\delta_{2},

B_{7}(t):=B_{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Here we also used
\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\simeq\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}.

3.9.
Energy estimate dependent on
\sigma

In order to construct the solution, we also establish an energy estimate without losing analyticity. We treat the last terms on the right-hand sides of (
3.104
)(
3.105
) in an alternative way:

(3.108)

\displaystyle\left[\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cb_{2}^{-1}\left[\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)+b_{2}\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cb_{2}^{-1}(B_{5}\delta_{2})^{2}\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+b_{2}\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Meanwhile, in view of (
3.103
), it holds

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{(2\sigma)^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left|\frac{\Delta\partial_{x}^{k}\theta(x,x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right|^{2}dxd\alpha\geq 2\pi\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}-\pi\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

This implies

(3.109)

\text{Diss}_{0}^{2}+\text{Diss}_{k}^{2}\gtrsim\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}-C\sigma^{-1}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Choosing
b_{2}
small enough and inserting (
3.108
)(
3.109
) into (
3.104
) and (
3.105
) yields that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(B_{6}-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\right)\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\sigma^{-1}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\left(B_{7}-\frac{c_{0}}{C_{4}}\right)\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

where
C_{4}
is a constant. If we let
\gamma(t)=\tilde{\gamma}
be a positive constant, then

(3.110)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\tilde{\gamma}}}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\tilde{\gamma}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{6}\left(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\tilde{\gamma}}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\tilde{\gamma}}}\right)+\sigma^{-1}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\tilde{\gamma}}}+\left(B_{7}-\frac{c_{0}}{C_{4}}\right)\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\tilde{\gamma}}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\tilde{\gamma}}}\right).

4.
Control for distance function

As presented by the coefficients
B_{6}
and
B_{7}
of (
3.107
), to close the energy estimate, we still need to find a control for
\sigma^{-1}\theta
. To this end, we set
\theta_{1}:=\sigma^{-1}\theta
and let
k_{1}:=k-3
. Notice that
k_{1}\geq\tilde{k}+2
since
k\geq 10
. Denote by
{N_{i}^{\theta}}^{\prime}
the commutators obtained from replacing
k
by
k_{1}
in the expressions
{N_{i}^{\theta}}
. Then we replace
k
by
k_{1}
in the equation (
3.2
) and multiply it by
\sigma^{-1}
to obtain

(4.1)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}+(\mu_{1}u_{+}+\mu_{2}u_{-})\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}\theta_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Delta\rho\,\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left[P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sigma^{-1}\left(\mu_{2}(P_{11}-P_{21})-\mu_{1}(P_{22}-P_{12})\right)(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right]

\displaystyle-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Delta\rho\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(P_{11}+P_{22}-P_{12}-P_{21}\right)(x,x-\alpha)\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}\theta_{1}(x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-1}\left({N_{2}^{\theta}}^{\prime}+{N_{4}^{\theta}}^{\prime}\right).

Here we note that

\displaystyle\Delta\rho\,\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left[P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\mu_{2}(P_{11}-P_{21})-\mu_{1}(P_{22}-P_{12})\right)(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Delta\rho\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\mu_{2}(P_{11}-P_{21})-\mu_{1}(P_{22}-P_{12})\right)(x,x-\alpha)\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}h(x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle-(u_{+}-u_{-})\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}h+{N^{\theta}_{1}}^{\prime}+{N^{\theta}_{3}}^{\prime}.

Then we multiply (
4.1
) by
\overline{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}}
and integrate the real part over
\Gamma_{\pm(t)}
to get the equation of
\frac{d}{dt}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}
.
In fact, the estimates for the contribution of the terms
(\mu_{1}u_{+}+\mu_{2}u_{-})\partial_{x}^{{k_{1}}+1}\theta_{1}
,
\sigma^{-1}\left({N_{2}^{\theta}}^{\prime}+{N_{4}^{\theta}}^{\prime}\right)
and
-\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Delta\rho\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(P_{11}+P_{22}-P_{12}-P_{21}\right)(x,x-\alpha)\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}\theta_{1}(x-\alpha)d\alpha
are already available. First, using (
3.66
) with
u=u_{+}
and
u=u_{-}
immediately yields

(4.2)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(\mu_{1}u_{+}+\mu_{2}u_{-})\partial_{x}^{{k_{1}}+1}\theta_{1}\overline{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}}\,dx\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+B_{4}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2},

In view of (
3.6
)(
3.7
)(
3.8
), we have

(4.3)

\displaystyle-\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}P.V.\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(P_{11}+P_{22}-P_{12}-P_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}\theta_{1}(x_{1})dx_{1}\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}(x)}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Re\left(K_{11}-\frac{1}{2}K_{12}-\frac{1}{2}K_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1},

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Re\left(K_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{K}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{K}_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}.

\displaystyle-\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(J_{11}+J_{22}\right)(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}(x)}dxdx_{1}.

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}(J_{12}+J_{21}+\tilde{J}_{12}+\tilde{J}_{21})(x,x_{1})\cdot\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\cdot\overline{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}(x)}dxdx_{1}.

From the proof of (
3.39
), the first and second terms can be estimated as follows.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Re\left(K_{11}-\frac{1}{2}K_{12}-\frac{1}{2}K_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1},

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\Re\left(K_{22}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{K}_{12}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{K}_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle-C\|\partial_{x}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

By (
3.43
)(
3.44
), the absolute value of the last two terms can be bounded by

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\left\|\theta_{1}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\|\partial_{x}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Taking the sum yields

(4.4)

\displaystyle-\Re\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}P.V.\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\left(P_{11}+P_{22}-P_{12}-P_{21}\right)(x,x_{1})\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}\theta_{1}(x_{1})dx_{1}\,\overline{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{c_{0}}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\left\|\theta_{1}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\|\partial_{x}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

For
{N_{2}^{\theta}}^{\prime}+{N_{4}^{\theta}}^{\prime}
, recall that

\displaystyle(\Delta\rho)^{-1}({N_{2}^{\theta}}^{\prime}+{N_{4}^{\theta}}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}},(P_{11}+P_{22}-P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}\theta(x)-\partial_{x}\theta(x-\alpha))d\alpha

\displaystyle+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\,P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}},(\mu_{2}P_{21}+\mu_{1}P_{12})(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha.

The first term on the right-hand side can be controlled as follows by completely repeating the argument for the safe and easy terms in class (i) and class (ii), see (
3.69
)(
3.73
-
3.75
)(
3.79
)(
3.92
).

(4.5)

\displaystyle\left\|P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}},(P_{11}+P_{22}-P_{12}-P_{21})(x,x-\alpha)](\partial_{x}\theta(x)-\partial_{x}\theta(x-\alpha))d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{5}\delta_{2}\|\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+B_{5}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x-\alpha)|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{j}\theta|^{2}d\alpha dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+B_{5}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{5}h\|_{H^{k_{1}-5}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{5}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-5}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Here
\tilde{k}_{1}:=\left\lfloor\frac{k_{1}+1}{2}\right\rfloor
, and we used
\tilde{k}_{1}\leq\tilde{k}
.
The second term is a linear combination of

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x),

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)

for
1\leq l\leq j\leq k_{1}
and
r\in S_{j,l}
. Suppose first
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}_{1}
. Completely repeating the argument for the safe and easy terms in class (iii) in the case
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}
(see (
3.76
)(
3.78
)(
3.97
)), we already have

(4.6)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\|\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Otherwise, there exists
a_{0}>\tilde{k}_{1}
such that
r_{a_{0}}\neq 0
and thus
j>\tilde{k}_{1}
,
r_{a_{0}}=1
,
r_{a}=0
for all
a>\tilde{k}_{1}
with
a\neq a_{0}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}\,d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\,d\alpha.

The
L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
norm of the second term can be controlled in the same way as in (
3.98
) or (
3.96
) by

\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}\|\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

For the first term, we will not follow (
3.101
) since it cannot provide the factor
\|\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}
in order to get a
\sigma-
independent estimate of
\theta_{1}
. Instead, we have to use the technique in (
3.87
) and (
3.90
). To be specific, we use the decomposition

\displaystyle q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha^{-l}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)\right)^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g\right)^{r_{a}}\right]\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x),

The first term disappears if
l=1
. Recall that

\displaystyle q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)=\frac{(-i)^{l}l!}{2}\left[\frac{\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}+\frac{(-1)^{l}\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}\right].

As an analogue of (
3.87
), if
l\geq 2
,
there is

(4.7)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left[\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)\right)^{r_{a}}-\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g\right)^{r_{a}}\right]\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{(\alpha\pm i(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)))^{l+1}}d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-2}\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}+1}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

For the second term, replacing
\partial_{x}^{k+2-j}h
by
\partial_{x}^{a_{0}+1}g
and replacing
\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}\theta
by
\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta
in (
3.90
) gives

(4.8)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a\neq a_{0}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a_{0}}g}{\alpha}d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{l-1}\|\partial_{x}^{a_{0}+1}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Note that
a_{0}
reaches
k_{1}
if
l=1
and
j=k_{1}
. Collecting (
4.6
-
4.8
) yields

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}g+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

The similar control holds for
\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-1}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}\theta(x)\,d\alpha
if we replace the derivatives of
g
by the derivatives of
f
on the right-hand side of the above inequality.
Then we conclude

(4.9)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}},(\mu_{2}P_{21}+\mu_{1}P_{12})(x,x-\alpha)]\partial_{x}\theta(x)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle B_{5}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

To bound
\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left[P.V.\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sigma^{-1}\left(\mu_{2}(P_{11}-P_{21})-\mu_{1}(P_{22}-P_{12})\right)(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right]
, observe that

\left(P_{11}-P_{21}\right)(x,x-\alpha)=\frac{\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}-2\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))\tilde{\Delta}f}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f)^{2})(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{2})},

\left(P_{22}-P_{12}\right)(x,x-\alpha)=\frac{\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}+2\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))\tilde{\Delta}g}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g)^{2})(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}g+2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2})}.

We present the details of the
L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
bound over
\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{11}-P_{21})(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right)
, and the bound over
\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{22}-P_{12})(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right)
can be obtained in a similar way. For simplicity, denote

Q_{1}(x,x-\alpha):=\frac{\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f)^{2})(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{2})},

Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha):=\frac{-2\alpha(2\sigma+\theta(x))\tilde{\Delta}f}{(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f)^{2})(\alpha^{2}+(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{2})}.

We also note that

Q_{1}(x,x-\alpha)=\alpha^{-3}(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right),

Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)=-2\alpha^{-3}\tilde{\Delta}f(2\sigma+\theta(x))q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right).

It suffices to bound the
L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
norm of
\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(Q_{i}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\right)d\alpha
,
i=1,2
. First, we write

\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}(Q_{i}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h)=\sum_{j=0}^{k_{1}}\binom{k_{1}}{j}\partial_{x}^{j}(Q_{i}(x,x-\alpha))\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}h.

\partial_{x}^{j}(Q_{1}(x,x-\alpha))
is a linear combination of terms in the form

\displaystyle\alpha^{-3}\partial_{x}^{j_{1}}\left[(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right]\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\right]\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]

with
j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j
. Hence it suffices to bound

\displaystyle Q_{1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}(x):=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-3}\partial_{x}^{j_{1}}\left[(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right]\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\right]\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}h\,d\alpha

for each possible index
(j_{1},j_{2},j_{3})
. To start with, we prepare some inequalities. As before, assume that (
3.17
) holds.
A simple computation gives

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{j}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq l\leq j}\sum_{S_{j,l}}C_{j,l,r}\,q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq l\leq j}\sum_{S_{j,l}}C_{j,l,r}\,\frac{(-i)^{l}l!}{2}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)\right)^{r_{a}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\frac{\alpha}{(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{l+1}}+\frac{(-1)^{l}\alpha}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{l+1}}\right).

\left|\frac{\alpha}{(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{l+1}}+\frac{(-1)^{l}\alpha}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x))^{l+1}}\right|\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\frac{1}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{\frac{l}{2}}}.

If
(j,l)\neq(k_{1},1)
and
j\geq 1
, then
j\leq k_{1}-1
or
r_{k_{1}}=0
, and subsequently

(4.10)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{\frac{l}{2}}}\prod_{a=1}^{j}\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)\right)^{r_{a}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j-1}.

If
(j,l)=(k_{1},1)
,

(4.11)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f-\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)}{(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2})^{\frac{l}{2}}}\right|\lesssim\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f}{\alpha}\right|+\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|.

For all
1\leq j\leq k_{1}
, using the embedding
\|\cdot\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\cdot\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}
, it can be shown that

(4.12)

\left|\partial_{x}^{j}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\right]\right|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j-1}.

\partial_{x}^{j}(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}
can be controlled as follows:

(4.13)

\left|\partial_{x}^{j}(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\sigma+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),\;1\leq j\leq k_{1}-1.

(4.14)

\left|\partial_{x}^{j}(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}\right|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\sigma+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\sigma|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|,\quad j=k_{1}.

When
j=0
, we simply use
\left|q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\right|\lesssim 1
,
|2\sigma+\theta(x)|^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}
and
\left|q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}
. Suppose now
j_{3}\neq k_{1}
,
j_{1}\neq k_{1}
. In this case, we use (
4.10
)(
4.12
)(
4.13
) to obtain

\displaystyle\|Q_{1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{2}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}d\alpha\left\|M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+2-j}h]\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}},

where we note that
\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{j}_{\gamma(t)}}
for
j\geq 0
and denote

\displaystyle B_{8}(t):=

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{2}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}.

In the case
j_{3}=k_{1}
, we must have
j_{1}=j_{2}=0
,
j=k_{1}
. Using the inequality

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha^{-3}(2\sigma+\theta(x))^{2}q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f}{\alpha}\right|+\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}

and (
4.10
)(
4.11
) gives that

\begin{aligned} \|Q_{1}^{k_{1},0,0,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim&\sigma B_{8}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.\end{aligned}

In the case
j_{1}=j=k_{1}
, we must have
j_{2}=j_{3}=0
. By (
4.14
), it holds

\displaystyle\|Q_{1}^{k_{1},k_{1},0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sigma}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}d\alpha\|M[\partial_{x}^{2}h]\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sigma|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h}{\alpha}\right|d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Taking the sum, we conclude that

(4.15)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}(Q_{1}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

In addition, since
|Q_{1}(x,x-\alpha)|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\alpha|}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}
, we also have

(4.16)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}Q_{1}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Next, we are devoted to the control of
\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}(Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}
.
\partial_{x}^{j}(Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha))
is a linear combination of terms in the form

q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha):=\alpha^{-3}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{j_{4}}f\cdot\partial_{x}^{j_{1}}(2\sigma+\theta(x))\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\right]\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]

with
j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j
.
We decompose the integral over
\alpha
into the parts
|\alpha|\leq 1
and
|\alpha|>1
. To be specific, we need to control

\displaystyle Q_{2,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x):=\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}h\,d\alpha,

\displaystyle Q_{2,|\alpha|>1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x):=\int_{|\alpha|>1}q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}h\,d\alpha.

In the region
|\alpha|>1
, if
j_{1}\neq k_{1}
and
j_{3}\neq k_{1}
, by (
4.10
)(
4.12
) and

\left|\alpha^{-1}\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{j_{4}}f\right|\lesssim M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+1}f](x-\alpha)
, there is

\displaystyle\left|q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\sigma+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}\frac{M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+1}f](x-\alpha)}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}},

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,|\alpha|>1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+1}f](x-\alpha)M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+2-j}h](x)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Since
0\leq j_{4}\leq j\leq k_{1}
, it follows

\|Q_{2,|\alpha|>1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

If
j_{3}=j=k_{1}
, then
j_{1}=j_{2}=j_{4}=0
. Using (
4.10
)(
4.11
) and that

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\alpha^{-3}\tilde{\Delta}f(2\sigma+\theta(x))q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f}{\alpha}\right|+\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}M[\partial_{x}f](x-\alpha)M[\partial_{x}^{2}h](x-\alpha)\left(M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x)+\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

we can also obtain
\|Q_{2,|\alpha|>1}^{k_{1},0,0,k_{1},0}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

If
j_{1}=j=k_{1}
, then
j_{2}=j_{3}=j_{4}=0
, and

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,|\alpha|>1}^{k_{1},k_{1},0,0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}M[\partial_{x}f](x-\alpha)M[\partial_{x}^{2}h](x-\alpha)|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Taking the sum yields

(4.17)

\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}(Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

In addition, it can be shown easily that

(4.18)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|>1}Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

In the region
|\alpha|\leq 1
, we use the decomposition

\displaystyle Q_{2,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x)=

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1-j}h-\alpha\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+2-j}h(x))\,d\alpha

\displaystyle+\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\alpha q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+2-j}h(x)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle Q_{2,1,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x)+Q_{2,2,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x).

For the first term, in the case
j_{1}\neq k_{1}
,
j_{3}\neq k_{1}
, (
4.10
) and (
4.12
) yield that

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,1,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\sigma+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\frac{M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+1}f](x-\alpha)}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\alpha^{2}M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+3-j}h](x)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}

If
j_{3}=j=k_{1}
, then
j_{1}=j_{2}=j_{4}=0
. By (
4.11
), it holds

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,1,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},0,0,k_{1},0}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+\sigma\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

If
j_{1}=j=k_{1}
, then
j_{2}=j_{3}=j_{4}=0
, and

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,1,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},k_{1},0,0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\frac{\alpha^{2}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}M[\partial_{x}f](x-\alpha)M[\partial_{x}^{3}h](x)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Summing the bounds for the above three cases gives

(4.19)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h-\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right)\right)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Meanwhile, we also have

(4.20)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)\left(\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h-\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right)\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

To bound
Q_{2,2,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}
in
L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}
norm, we write

\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\alpha q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)d\alpha=\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\alpha\left(q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)-q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x+\alpha)\right)d\alpha.

To use Lemma
3.5
, we need the control over the difference between each factor of
q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x\pm\alpha)
.

Lemma 4.1
.

Suppose condition (
3.17
) holds. Then for all
1\leq j_{2}\leq k_{1}
,

(4.21)

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right)\right]-\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{j_{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j_{2}}\sum_{a=0}^{j_{2}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)\right).

For
j_{3}=k_{1}
,

(4.22)

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]-\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\sigma+\alpha^{2}\sum_{a=0}^{k_{1}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sum_{a=1}^{k_{1}}|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{|\alpha|}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sigma\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left[|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x+\alpha)\right)+|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right].

For
1\leq j_{3}\leq k_{1}-1
,

(4.23)

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]-\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j_{3}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\sigma+\alpha^{2}\sum_{a=0}^{j_{3}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f]](x+\alpha)\right)+\sum_{a=1}^{j_{3}}|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)|\right].

Proof.

To prove (
4.21
), first recall that

\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\right]=\sum_{l=1}^{j_{2}}\sum_{S_{j_{2},l}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j_{2}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}.

Then using Lemma
3.5
, the embedding
\|\cdot\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\cdot\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}
and the inequalities

\left|\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}f(x)-\partial_{x}^{a}f(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}-\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{a}f(x)}{\alpha}\right|\lesssim|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)\right),

\displaystyle\left|q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right|\lesssim|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)\right),

we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}\right)\right]-\partial_{x}^{j_{2}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{j_{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j_{2}}\sum_{a=0}^{j_{2}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)\right).

To show (
4.22
), we note that

\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]=\sum_{l=1}^{j_{3}}\sum_{S_{j_{3},l}}q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j_{3}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{a}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}},

\displaystyle q^{(l)}\left(\frac{\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)=\frac{(-i)^{l}l!}{2}\left[\frac{\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha+i(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)))^{l+1}}+\frac{(-1)^{l}\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha-i(\tilde{\Delta}f-2\sigma-\theta(x)))^{l+1}}\right],

(4.24)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}f(x)-\partial_{x}^{a}f(x-\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}-\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{a}f(x)+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\left|\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right|.

Moreover, in order to bound
q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha+i(f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)))^{l+1}}-\frac{\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha-i(f(x)-f(x+\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)))^{l+1}}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{(-1)^{l+1}\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha-i(f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)))^{l+1}}-\frac{(-1)^{l+1}\alpha^{l+1}}{(\alpha+i(f(x)-f(x+\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)))^{l+1}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{l+1}\left(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-\frac{l+2}{2}}\left|2f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-f(x+\alpha)-4\sigma-2\theta(x)\right|.

Therefore,

(4.25)

\displaystyle\left|q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)-q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{l+1}\left(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-\frac{l+2}{2}}\left[|\alpha|^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sigma\right].

If
(j_{3},l)\neq(k_{1},1)
, then
j_{3}\leq k_{1}-1
or
r_{k_{1}}=0
, using (
4.24
)(
4.25
) and Lemma
3.5
, we obtain

(4.26)

\displaystyle\left|q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j_{3}}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}f(x)-\partial_{x}^{a}f(x-\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-q^{(l)}\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\prod_{a=1}^{j_{3}}\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{a}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{a}f(x)+\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)^{r_{a}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sigma\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{l-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{r_{a}\neq 0}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f]](x+\alpha)\right)+|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)|\right].

In particular, when
1\leq j_{3}\leq k_{1}-1
, it follows

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]-\partial_{x}^{j_{3}}\left[q\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j_{3}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\sigma+\alpha^{2}\sum_{a=0}^{j_{3}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f]](x+\alpha)\right)+\sum_{a=1}^{j_{3}}|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)|\right].

If
(j_{3},l)=(k_{1},1)
, then
r_{a}=0
for all
a\leq k_{1}-1
and
r_{k_{1}}=1
,

\displaystyle\left|q^{(1)}\left(\frac{f(x)-f(x-\alpha)-2\sigma-\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f(x)-\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f(x-\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-q^{(1)}\left(\frac{f(x+\alpha)-f(x)+2\sigma+\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\left(\frac{\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f(x)+\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)}{\alpha}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{|\alpha|}{\left(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sigma\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left[|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x+\alpha)\right)+|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right].

Taking the sum with (
4.26
) yields (
4.22
).
∎

Now, using Lemma
3.5
, Lemma
4.23
together with the bound (
4.10
-
4.12
) and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial_{x}^{j_{4}}f(x)-\partial_{x}^{j_{4}}f(x-\alpha)}{\alpha}-\frac{\partial_{x}^{j_{4}}f(x+\alpha)-\partial_{x}^{j_{4}}f(x)}{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+2}f](x+\alpha)\right),

we are able to obtain the control over
q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)-q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x+\alpha)
. In the case
j_{1}\neq k_{1}
,
j_{3}\neq k_{1}
, by the Sobolev embedding
|\partial_{x}^{j}\theta(x)|\lesssim\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}
,
k_{1}-1\geq j\geq 1
, there is

\displaystyle|\alpha|\left|q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x-\alpha)-q_{2}^{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}(x,x+\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\left(1+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{j_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{j_{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j_{2}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{j_{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{j_{3}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{j_{3}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\frac{\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{j_{4}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\alpha^{2}\sum_{a=0}^{j_{2}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{j_{4}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left[\sigma+\alpha^{2}\sum_{a=0}^{j_{3}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f]](x+\alpha)\right)+\sum_{a=1}^{j_{3}}|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)|\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{j_{4}+2}f](x+\alpha)\right)\right\},

and subsequently

(4.27)

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,2,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{j_{4}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+2-j}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

In the case
j_{1}=j=k_{1},j_{2}=j_{3}=j_{4}=0
, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha|\left|q_{2}^{k_{1},0,0,0}(x,x-\alpha)-q_{2}^{k_{1},0,0,0}(x,x+\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\left\{\frac{\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left[|\alpha|^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sigma\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)\right\},

(4.28)

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,2,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},k_{1},0,0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

In the case
j_{3}=j=k_{1}
,
j_{1}=j_{2}=j_{4}=0
, by (

)(

) and (

),

\displaystyle|\alpha|\left|q_{2}^{0,0,k_{1},0}(x,x-\alpha)-q_{2}^{0,0,k_{1},0}(x,x+\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}\frac{\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\left\{\sigma+\alpha^{2}\sum_{a=0}^{k_{1}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sum_{a=1}^{k_{1}}|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)|\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sigma\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left[|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x+\alpha)\right)+|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right].\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sigma\alpha^{2}}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left[M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)^{k_{1}}+\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f}{\alpha}\right|+\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right]

Then we bound the contribution from each term on the right-hand side:

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left[\sigma+\alpha^{2}\sum_{a=0}^{k_{1}}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{a+2}f](x+\alpha)\right)\right]d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\frac{\sigma}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\sum_{a=1}^{k_{1}}|\partial_{x}^{a}\theta(x)|d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}-1}_{\gamma(t)}},

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\left(\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}\right)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)+\sigma\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left[|\alpha|\left(M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f](x+\alpha)\right)+|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right]d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+1\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\frac{\sigma\alpha^{2}\left[M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right]}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}d\alpha\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}},

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\frac{\sigma\alpha^{2}\left[M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right]}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left(\left|\frac{\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}f}{\alpha}\right|+\sigma^{-1}|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta(x)|\right)d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}+1}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Taking the sum and using Sobolev embedding yields

(4.29)

\displaystyle\|Q_{2,2,|\alpha|\leq 1}^{k_{1},0,0,k_{1},0}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

(
4.27
-
4.29
) imply that

\left\|\int_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\alpha\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right)d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}
,
which together with (

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{11}-P_{21})(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Moreover, by Lemma
3.5
again, we have

\displaystyle\left|\alpha||Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)-Q_{2}(x,x+\alpha)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma}{\alpha^{2}+(2\sigma)^{2}}\left[\alpha^{2}\left(M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x-\alpha)+M[\partial_{x}^{2}f](x+\alpha)\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+\sigma\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right],

and subsequently

(4.31)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0\leq\alpha\leq 1}\alpha\left(Q_{2}(x,x-\alpha)-Q_{2}(x,x+\alpha)\right)d\alpha\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}h(x)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Summing up (

)(

)(

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(P_{11}-P_{21}\right)(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

As an analogue, it can also be shown

(4.33)

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}(P_{22}-P_{12})(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma B_{8}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

(4.34)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(P_{22}-P_{12}\right)(x,x-\alpha)\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}h\,d\alpha\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\lesssim\sigma\left(1+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}.

Finally, we insert the controls (

)(

)(

)(

)(

)(

) into (

) and conclude with the estimate over

:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+c_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Delta\rho\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})\left|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\right|^{2}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(B_{5}+w_{0})\left(\delta_{2}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{j=0}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+B_{7}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\theta_{1}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}

\displaystyle+B_{9}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+(B_{5}\delta_{2}+B_{4}-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t)))\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2},

where

B_{9}(t):=B_{5}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta_{1}\|_{H^{\tilde{k}_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)+B_{8}\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

Meanwhile, we can also estimate
\frac{d}{dt}\|\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}
through (

)(
4.34
):

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+c_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Delta\rho\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\tilde{\Delta}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\|\theta_{1}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(B_{5}+w_{0})\left(\delta_{2}+\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm}(t)}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})|\tilde{\Delta}\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}dxdx_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+B_{7}\|\theta_{1}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\left(1+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}\|\theta_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}

\displaystyle+(B_{5}\delta_{2}+B_{4}-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t)))\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}.

Summing the above two inequalities and using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality for the first terms on the right-hand sides like what we have done in (
3.106
), we obtain

(4.35)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{2}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\Delta\rho\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}\int_{\Gamma_{\pm(t)}}D_{22}^{0}(x,x_{1})\left(\Delta\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\theta_{1}|^{2}+|\Delta\theta_{1}|^{2}\right)dxdx_{1}

\displaystyle-\left(2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))+C_{3}B_{7}\right)\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(B_{6}-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))\right)\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+B_{9}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}},

In addition, if we let
\gamma(t)=\tilde{\gamma}
be a positive constant, then by (
3.109
)

(4.36)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(B_{6}+\sigma^{-1}\right)\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\left(B_{7}-\frac{c_{0}}{C_{4}}\right)\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}

\displaystyle+B_{9}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}h\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}\theta\|_{H^{k_{1}+1}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}},

5.
Proof of theorem
2.2

The proof of Theorem
2.2
will be completed through a bootstrap argument encompassing conditions (
2.20
) and (
2.21
). For simplicity, denote
E(t):=\|h(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\theta_{1}(t)\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}
. First, since we choose
k\geq 10
, it holds
k_{1}=k-3\geq\tilde{k}+2
and consequently

B_{3}\leq C_{5}\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),\;B_{4}\lesssim\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)E^{\frac{1}{2}},\;B_{5}\lesssim\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{k},\;\delta_{2}\lesssim E^{\frac{1}{2}},\;B_{8}\lesssim\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2k_{1}+2},

where we used Sobolev embedding, and
C_{5}
denotes the implicit constant. Again, by Sobolev embedding, it holds that
\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq C_{6}E(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}
with a constant
C_{6}
, from which it follows

(5.1)

\displaystyle B_{6}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2k}\left(\delta_{2}^{2}+\sigma+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\right)+B_{4}+\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{k}\delta_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2k+1}\left(E^{\frac{1}{2}}+\sigma\right).

(5.2)

B_{7}\leq C_{5}\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\leq C_{5}C_{6}\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)E^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.3)

B_{9}\lesssim\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2k-3}.

In one hand, if we let
\gamma(t)=\gamma(0)
be invariant in time and define

\tilde{E}(t):=\|h(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(0)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(0)}}^{2}+\|\theta_{1}(t)\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(0)}}^{2},

then by (
3.110
)(
4.36
), as long as (
3.17
)(
3.70
) hold with
\gamma(t)=\gamma(0)
, there is

\displaystyle\frac{d\tilde{E}}{dt}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(B_{6}+B_{9}+\sigma^{-1}\right)\tilde{E}+\left(B_{7}-\frac{c_{0}}{C_{4}}\right)\left(\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(t)}}\right),

where
B_{6}
,
B_{7}
,
B_{9}
satisfy the bounds (
5.1
-
5.3
) with
E
replaced by
\tilde{E}
. Moreover, if we assume in addition that

(5.4)

B_{7}-\frac{c_{0}}{C_{4}}\leq 0,

then it follows that

(5.5)

\frac{d\tilde{E}}{dt}\lesssim\left(\mathcal{C}(\tilde{E})+\sigma^{-1}\right)\tilde{E},

where
\mathcal{C}(E)
is a smooth function of
E
with the bound
\mathcal{C}(E)\lesssim\left(1+E^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2k+2}
. On the other hand, since
f=h+\mu_{1}\theta
,
g=h-\mu_{2}\theta
, there exists a constant
C_{7}
such that

\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|_{H^{1}_{\gamma(t)}}+\sigma^{-1}\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq C_{7}E^{\frac{1}{2}}.

In view of (
2.18
)(
2.19
), by choosing
\epsilon_{0}
,
\epsilon_{1}
small enough, we can guarantee that

(5.6)

E(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}<c_{1}:=\min\left\{1,\frac{\delta_{1}}{C_{7}},\frac{w_{0}}{C_{7}},\frac{c_{0}}{2C_{4}C_{5}C_{6}}\right\}

holds for
t=0
, and thus (
3.17
)(
3.70
)(
5.4
) hold at
t=0
. Then using the a priori estimate (
5.5
), it can be shown in a standard way of iteration and compactness argument that there exists a unique solution
(h,\theta)\in H^{k}_{\gamma(0)}\times H^{k}_{\gamma(0)}
on
[0,T_{0}]
with
T_{0}\gtrsim\sigma
. In fact, if we consider the maximal interval
[0,T_{0}]
on which (
5.6
) holds, then for
t\in[0,T_{0}]
, we have
\mathcal{C}(E)\lesssim 1
. Subsequently, for certain constant
C^{\prime}

\tilde{E}(t)\leq\exp\left(C^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\sigma^{-1})ds\right)E(0)\leq\exp(C^{\prime}(1+\sigma^{-1})t)E(0).

Choose
T_{\sigma,0}=\frac{\sigma}{3C^{\prime}}\log\left(\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{E(0)}\right)
, then
\tilde{E}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}<c_{1}
for all
t\in[0,T_{\sigma,0}]
, and thus
T_{0}\geq T_{\sigma,0}
. Such control can be used to construct the solution
(h,\theta)\in L^{\infty}\left([0,T_{\sigma,0}];H^{k}_{\gamma(0)}\right)\times L^{\infty}\left([0,T_{\sigma,0}];H^{k}_{\gamma(0)}\right)
.

Next, observing that
(h,\theta)\in L^{\infty}\left([0,T_{\sigma,0}];H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}\right)\times L^{\infty}\left([0,T_{\sigma,0}];H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}\right)
and

E(t)\leq\tilde{E}(t)<c_{1}
for any decreasing positive function
\gamma(t)
, we are able to define the function
\gamma(t)
by solving (
2.22
):

-\frac{d\log(\cosh(2\gamma(t))}{dt}=-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))=C_{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

with
\gamma(0)=\gamma_{0}
. Here
C_{2}:=2C_{3}C_{5}
so that

C_{3}B_{7}\leq C_{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)=-2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t)).

Since
\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq C_{6}E(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{6}\tilde{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)<C_{6}
on
[0,T_{\sigma,0}]
, such
\gamma
can be defined at least on
[0,T_{\sigma,0}]\cap[0,(C_{2}C_{6})^{-1}\log(\cosh(2\gamma_{0}))].

Now, we impose the bootstrap assumptions. Suppose that the solution and
\gamma(t)
exist on
[0,\tilde{T}]
with the following assumptions:

(i)

The function
\gamma(t)
satisfies (
2.22
):

2\gamma^{\prime}(t)\tanh(2\gamma(t))=-C_{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right).

(ii)

E(t)
satisfies the bound (
5.6
).

By (
3.107
)(
4.35
) and (
2.22
), we have

(5.7)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)\lesssim\left(B_{6}+B_{7}\right)\left(\|h\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)

(5.8)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\theta_{1}\|_{H^{k-3}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\lesssim\left(B_{6}+B_{7}+B_{9}\right)E(t).

In view of (
5.1
-
5.3
) and assumption (
5.6
), we have
B_{6}+B_{7}+B_{9}\lesssim 1
on
[0,\tilde{T}]
. Therefore,

(5.9)

\|h(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\leq e^{C_{0}t}\epsilon_{0}^{2},\quad\|\theta_{1}(t)\|_{H^{k-3}_{\gamma(t)}}^{2}\leq E(t)\leq e^{C_{1}t}\left(\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\right)

for
t\in[0,\tilde{T}]
and large enough
C_{0}
,
C_{1}
. Now we let
T_{1}
be such that
e^{C_{0}T_{1}}\epsilon_{0}^{2}+e^{C_{1}T_{1}}\left(\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\right)=\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{2}.

If we already have
\tilde{T}\geq T_{1}
, then the proof is complete. Hence we assume without loss of generality that
\tilde{T}<T_{1}
. In this case, we have
E(t)\leq\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{2}
for all
t\in[0,\tilde{T}]
. Now we let
\tilde{E}(t):=\|h(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(\tilde{T})}}^{2}+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\|\theta(t)\|_{H^{k}_{\gamma(\tilde{T})}}^{2}+\|\theta_{1}(t)\|_{H^{k_{1}}_{\gamma(\tilde{T})}}^{2}
and invoke (
3.110
)(
4.36
) and thus (
5.5
). By repeating the argument we used to construct the solution on
[0,T_{\sigma,0}]
, we can extend the solution to
[0,\tilde{T}+T_{\sigma}]
with
T_{\sigma}=\frac{\sigma}{3C^{\prime}}\log\left(\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{E(\tilde{T})}\right)\geq\frac{\sigma\log 2}{3C^{\prime}}
. Moreover, it follows that for
t\in[\tilde{T},\tilde{T}+T_{\sigma}]

\tilde{E}(t)\leq\exp\left(C^{\prime}\int_{\tilde{T}}^{\tilde{T}+T_{\sigma}}(1+\sigma^{-1})ds\right)E(\tilde{T})<c_{1}.

To extend
\gamma
, we integrate (
2.22
) to get

\displaystyle\log\left(\cosh(2\gamma(\tilde{T}))\right)-\log\left(\cosh(2\gamma_{0})\right)=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\tilde{T}}C_{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\right)dt

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-C_{2}C_{6}\int_{0}^{\tilde{T}}E^{\frac{1}{2}}dt

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-C_{2}C_{6}c_{1}\tilde{T}.

Let
T_{2}>0
be such that
C_{2}C_{6}c_{1}T_{2}=\frac{1}{2}\log\left(\cosh(2\gamma_{0})\right)
. Without loss of generality, we assume
\tilde{T}<T_{2}
, and subsequently
\log\left(\cosh(2\gamma(\tilde{T}))\right)>\frac{1}{2}\log\left(\cosh(2\gamma_{0})\right)
. As before, noting that

\|\partial_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}+\|\partial_{x}\theta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\gamma(t)}}\leq C_{6}E(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{6}\tilde{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)<C_{6}
in
[\tilde{T},\tilde{T}+T_{\sigma}]
, we can then extend
\gamma(t)
to
[\tilde{T},\tilde{T}+T_{\sigma}]\cap[\tilde{T},\tilde{T}+(2C_{2}C_{6})^{-1}\log(\cosh(2\gamma_{0}))]
by solving (
2.22
) in this interval. So far, we have completed the extension of the bootstrap assumptions (i) and (ii) to
[0,\tilde{T}+T_{\sigma}]\cap[0,\tilde{T}+(2C_{2}C_{6})^{-1}\log(\cosh(2\gamma_{0}))].
Therefore, we conclude that (i) and (ii) hold until
\tilde{T}
reaches
T:=\min\left\{T_{1},T_{2}\right\}
. Moreover, (
5.9
) holds for all
t\leq T
, which gives (
2.20
)(
2.21
).∎
